## 2026-05-09 — US-044 (Phase 5a.7 — start_insider_round RPC)
- TDD red: wrote `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0023-start-insider-round.test.ts` (4 tests: happy-path host call returning round_number=1 with secret picked from football-premier-league pack, 1 master + 1 insider + 2 player roles for a 4-player room, room transitions LOBBY → PLAYING with current_round=1; non-host → PGAME12/PG012; status=PLAYING → PGAME13/PG013; 2-player room → PGAME14/PG014). Ran against schema with no migration 0023 — all 4 failed with `PGRST202 Could not find the function public.start_insider_round`, the expected red.
- TDD green: wrote `supabase/migrations/0023_start_insider_round.sql`:
  - `start_insider_round(p_room_id uuid, p_pack_slug text, p_time_limit_s int, p_player_id uuid) returns int` (returns the new round_number).
  - SECURITY DEFINER, `set search_path = public`.
  - Step 1: `select host_player_id, status into v_host, v_status from rooms where id = p_room_id for update` — single locked read.
  - Step 2: PGAME04/PG004 if room missing; PGAME12/PG012 if caller ≠ host.
  - Step 3: PGAME13/PG013 if status ≠ 'LOBBY'.
  - Step 4: `select count(*), array_agg(player_id order by random()) into v_player_count, v_player_ids from players where room_id = p_room_id`. PGAME14/PG014 if count < 3.
  - Step 5: `select display_value into v_secret from get_random_pack_item(p_pack_slug)` — propagates PGAME01/PG001 if pack unknown.
  - Step 6: `coalesce(max(round_number), 0) + 1` → next round number (always 1 in practice today; forward-compatible with a future next_insider_round RPC).
  - Step 7: insert into game_insider_round with phase='preparing'.
  - Step 8: insert role rows via `generate_subscripts(v_player_ids, 1) as g(i)`, mapping `i=1 → master`, `i=2 → insider`, else `player`.
  - Step 9: `update rooms set status='PLAYING', current_round = v_round_number`.
  - GRANT EXECUTE to anon.
- Ran `bunx supabase db reset`: all 23 migrations applied clean.
- 4/4 new tests pass; full workspace `bunx vitest run` reports 145/145 across 24 files (was 141/141 — added 4 new tests).
- Regenerated `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` via `bunx supabase gen types typescript --local 2>/dev/null > …`. Verified `start_insider_round` appears with `Args: { p_pack_slug, p_player_id, p_room_id, p_time_limit_s }, Returns: number`. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean across apps/insider, apps/headball, apps/hub.
- `bun run lint` passes (FULL TURBO cache hit on the 2 lint tasks).
- Updated `packages/core/error-codes.md`: bound PGAME12=only-host-can-start-round, PGAME13=room-not-in-lobby, PGAME14=fewer-than-3-players to migration 0023; added the SQLSTATE mapping rows; reorganized the "Insider codes" planning section to list bound codes first (PGAME11–14) and note PGAME10/15+ are still unassigned.
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0023_start_insider_round.sql` (new), `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0023-start-insider-round.test.ts` (new), `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` (regen), `packages/core/error-codes.md` (PGAME12/13/14 bindings).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Insider round bootstrap pattern (canonical shape):** captured at top of progress.txt. The five-step `for update` → host-gate → LOBBY-gate → shuffle-and-count → insert-round/insert-roles → flip-room-to-PLAYING shape is the canonical "host starts game" RPC. Reusable for any future game's start RPC; only the role-distribution step varies.
  - **Random shuffle via `array_agg(... order by random())` + `generate_subscripts`:** captured at top of progress.txt. Single-pass shuffle then index-based role mapping avoids any read-then-update races and keeps the SQL idiomatic and short. Will repeat for any future role-assignment RPC.
  - **`for update` row lock for atomic auth + status read:** locking the rooms row with `for update` is the same pattern Headball's `start_game` (migration 0004) uses. It guarantees that two concurrent host clicks can't both pass the LOBBY check — the second gets serialized and sees PLAYING. No need for advisory locks or extra ceremony.
  - **PGAME04 reuse for "room not found":** the cross-game `PGAME04 / PG004 = "room not found"` slot (reserved in error-codes.md but not yet bound) is now first-bound by `start_insider_round`. Future game-bootstrap RPCs that need the same gate should reuse PG004 rather than picking a new code.
  - **`returns int` round_number contract:** start_insider_round returns the new round_number (always 1 today via the LOBBY gate, but the `coalesce(max+1)` keeps the contract clean for a future "next round" path). Apps consume via `const { data: round } = await supabase.rpc('start_insider_round', {...})` and use `round` as the round_number for follow-up RPCs.

- TDD red: wrote `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0022-advance-to-asking.test.ts` (4 tests: any-player advances preparing→asking with started_at stamp; idempotent second call; not-in-room→PGAME11/PG011; already-asking→no-op). Ran against schema with no migration 0022 — all 4 failed with `PGRST202 Could not find the function public.advance_to_asking`, the expected red.
- TDD green: wrote `supabase/migrations/0022_advance_to_asking.sql`:
  - SECURITY DEFINER `advance_to_asking(p_room_id, p_round, p_player_id)` returning void.
  - Step 1: `perform reconcile_round_phase(p_room_id, p_round)` — self-heal expired deadlines (T-2.A).
  - Step 2: validate `players` membership (`if not exists (select 1 from players where room_id = ... and player_id = ...)` then raise `'PGAME11: …' using errcode = 'PG011'`).
  - Step 3: `update game_insider_round set phase='asking', started_at=now() where … and phase='preparing'` — phase-guard makes the call idempotent (zero rows on second call or wrong phase, silent no-op).
  - GRANT EXECUTE to anon. `set search_path = public` per SECURITY DEFINER convention.
- Ran `bunx supabase db reset`: all 22 migrations applied clean.
- 4/4 new tests pass; full workspace `bunx vitest run` reports 141/141 pass across 23 files (was 137/137 — added 4 new tests for this story).
- Regenerated `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` via `bunx supabase gen types typescript --local 2>/dev/null > …`. Verified `advance_to_asking` appears with `Args: { p_player_id: string; p_room_id: string; p_round: number }`, `Returns: undefined`. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean across apps/insider, apps/headball, apps/hub.
- `bun run lint` passes (realtime-publication script + eslint, both cached or green).
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0022_advance_to_asking.sql` (new), `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0022-advance-to-asking.test.ts` (new), `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` (regen).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Standard Insider RPC skeleton emerges (per T-3.B / C2.A):** `advance_to_asking` is the canonical example of the three-step Insider mutation RPC: reconcile → authorize → phase-guarded UPDATE. Captured at top of progress.txt. Subsequent RPCs (`mark_correct_guess`, `cast_vote`, `advance_to_reveal`, etc.) should mirror this skeleton; the only variation is the authorization predicate (any-player vs host-only) and the phase transition pair.
  - **PGAME11 binding assignment:** `packages/core/error-codes.md` listed PGAME11 as a planned "already voted" example, but the Phase 5 PRD US-043 binds it to "player not in room". The error-codes.md note is non-binding ("Specific assignments are made when the relevant migration lands"); this migration claims PGAME11 = not-in-room. Future Insider RPCs that need an "already voted" code should pick the next free PGAMExx slot. Captured at top of progress.txt.
  - **Idempotency via WHERE-clause phase filter, not IF/THEN:** the temptation in PL/pgSQL is to wrap the UPDATE in `if (select phase from … ) = 'preparing' then update …`. Don't — the WHERE clause does it cleaner: zero rows match on a second call, the UPDATE is a no-op, no error. This also avoids the read-then-write race where phase could change between the SELECT and the UPDATE.
  - **`expect(after.started_at).toBe(firstStartedAt)` assertion robustness:** the idempotency test reads started_at, sleeps 50ms, calls advance again, and asserts the stamp is unchanged. If the WHERE-clause guard were missing (or if the function blindly re-stamped), the second now() would be at least ~50ms later and the assertion would catch it. 50ms is enough headroom for clock-resolution noise but small enough that sub-ms test churn doesn't matter. Reusable pattern for any future "phase-guarded mutation" idempotency test.

## 2026-05-09 — US-040 (Phase 5a.3 — Migration 0019 game_insider_responses + game_insider_votes)
- TDD red: wrote `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0019-responses-votes.test.ts` (9 tests covering both tables) and ran it against the prior schema — all 9 failed with `PGRST205 Could not find the table 'public.game_insider_responses'/'game_insider_votes'`, confirming the failing-for-the-right-reason red.
- TDD green: wrote `supabase/migrations/0019_game_insider_responses_votes.sql`:
  - `game_insider_responses (id bigserial PK, room_id uuid FK→rooms ON DELETE CASCADE, round_number int, response text check yes/no/unsure, created_at timestamptz default now())`. Btree index `(room_id, round_number, id)` for response-feed paging.
  - `game_insider_votes (room_id, round_number, voter_player_id, voted_player_id, voted_at default now(), PK (room_id, round_number, voter_player_id))`. Both PKs and the room FK are sufficient as written; cascade on rooms cleans both child tables when a room is deleted.
  - RLS on, anon SELECT policy on each.
  - Both tables added to `supabase_realtime` publication (no column-list — these tables hold no asymmetric secrets, unlike `game_insider_round` from migration 0017).
- Ran `bunx supabase db reset`: all 19 migrations applied clean.
- 9/9 new tests pass; full workspace `bunx vitest run` reports 123/123 pass across 20 files.
- Regenerated `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` via `bunx supabase gen types typescript --local 2>/dev/null > ...` — both new tables visible in the type file. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean in apps/headball, apps/hub, apps/insider.
- `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` passes (confirms every `create table` decision is recorded).
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0019_game_insider_responses_votes.sql` (new), `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0019-responses-votes.test.ts` (new), `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` (regen).
- **Learnings:**
  - **No-secrets Insider sub-tables (per US-040 / A1.C):** `game_insider_responses` and `game_insider_votes` carry NO asymmetric secret — the response feed and vote tally are public to the room from the moment they're recorded. Therefore the column-level GRANT pattern from migration 0017 (which protects `secret_value`) does NOT apply: a plain `enable row level security` + permissive `for select to anon using (true)` policy + `alter publication supabase_realtime add table <t>` (no column list) is the correct shape. Only future Insider tables that carry asymmetric data (next: nothing planned in Phase 5a — the secret stays on game_insider_round) should reach for column-level RLS.
  - **bigserial PK in Realtime publications:** `game_insider_responses.id` is `bigserial primary key`. PostgreSQL's logical replication broadcasts INSERT events with the freshly-assigned id, so the response feed renders deterministically by `order id asc` on the client. No special handling needed in the publication; the default identity (`REPLICA IDENTITY DEFAULT` = primary key) is sufficient because the PK is just `id`. If a future migration changes the PK to a composite, set `replica identity full` to keep DELETE/UPDATE payloads complete.
  - **Compound PK as a uniqueness constraint:** `game_insider_votes` uses the (room_id, round_number, voter_player_id) triple as PK to enforce "one ballot per voter per round" at the database level. The `cast_vote` RPC (later story) will rely on this: `insert into game_insider_votes ... on conflict do nothing` will silently no-op if the voter already cast their ballot, letting the RPC return the canonical row idempotently rather than 23505-erroring. Reusable pattern for any "exactly-one-per-actor-per-period" Insider write.

## 2026-05-08 — US-004 (Phase 0.4 — tsconfig.base.json with strict mode and path aliases)
- Created `tsconfig.base.json` at workspace root: `target: ES2022`, `module: esnext`, `moduleResolution: bundler`, `strict: true`, `jsx: preserve`, `baseUrl: "."`, `paths: { "@social-hub/*": ["packages/*/src/index.ts"] }`. Also includes `lib`, `esModuleInterop`, `resolveJsonModule`, `isolatedModules`, `skipLibCheck`, `noEmit`, `incremental`, `allowJs`.
- Updated `apps/{hub,insider}/tsconfig.json` to extend `../../tsconfig.base.json` and only retain `plugins: [{ name: "next" }]` + `include`/`exclude`. Stripped redundant compilerOptions inherited from base.
- Created `packages/{core,ui,types,content}/tsconfig.json` extending base with `include: ["src/**/*.ts", "src/**/*.tsx"]`.
- Did NOT touch the root `tsconfig.json` — it stays Next-13-style for the legacy Headball app at root until Phase 1 relocates that code.
- Verified: `bunx tsc --noEmit` from workspace root passes; `bunx tsc --noEmit` inside each apps/* and packages/* passes; placed a temp probe file `apps/hub/path-probe.ts` importing all four `@social-hub/*` packages and confirmed it type-checked clean (then removed).
- Files changed: `tsconfig.base.json` (new); `apps/hub/tsconfig.json`, `apps/insider/tsconfig.json` (rewritten to extend base); `packages/{core,ui,types,content}/tsconfig.json` (new).
- **Learnings:**
  - In TS 4.1+ `paths` declared in an extended config are resolved relative to the base config's directory — so putting `baseUrl: "."` + `paths` in `tsconfig.base.json` at root means a consumer at `apps/hub/tsconfig.json` resolves `@social-hub/core` to `<root>/packages/core/src/index.ts` without re-declaring anything.
  - `include` and `exclude` are NOT inherited via `extends`; each consumer must declare its own. Keep them per-package.
  - `jsx` belongs in base (set to `preserve` for Next apps). React-only library packages don't render JSX in src yet, so `preserve` doesn't hurt them; if a package later needs `react-jsx` for tests, override locally.
  - The wildcard `paths` mapping `@social-hub/*` → `packages/*/src/index.ts` only resolves bare-package imports (`@social-hub/core`), not deep imports (`@social-hub/core/foo`). That matches D3's "barrel-export only" rule.

| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` | `https://<staging-ref>.supabase.co` | Production, Preview, Development |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` | (anon key from staging Supabase Settings → API) | Production, Preview, Development |

Tip: `vercel env add NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL preview` from the CLI is faster than the dashboard once each project is linked locally with `vercel link`.

**3. Trigger preview deploys**

Push the current `feat/multigame-platform` branch (already pushed at HEAD `045dcd6`). Each Vercel project will fire a preview deploy automatically. Wait for all three to go green.

Expected results:
- **hub preview** → renders "Hub placeholder" text (from `apps/hub/app/page.tsx`).
- **insider preview** → renders "Insider placeholder" text (from `apps/insider/app/page.tsx`).
- **headball preview** → builds (empty app for now; may show 404 on `/` since `apps/headball` has no pages yet — that's expected for Phase 0).

**4. Document preview URLs**

Paste the three preview URLs into the PR description for `feat/multigame-platform` (or save them in `.ralph/preview-urls.txt` for now if no PR yet).

**5. Flip the passes flag**

When all three previews are green and env vars are set on all three projects, edit `.ralph/prd.json` and change US-005's `"passes": false` → `"passes": true`. Then re-trigger the Ralph loop and it will advance to US-006.

### Why the agent cannot do this

- The Vercel dashboard is gated by browser auth; even `vercel project add` from CLI requires the project to first be linked to a personal/team scope, and the staging Supabase keys (US-006) live in a place the agent can't fetch programmatically.
- Per Ralph instructions ("Manual Operational Stories"), US-005 and US-006 are explicit pause points — the agent prints instructions and stops.

### Order-of-operations note

US-005 depends on US-006 (the staging Supabase env vars). Recommended order:
1. Do US-006 first (create staging Supabase project, run `bunx supabase db push --linked`, copy URL + anon key).
2. Then US-005 with those env values.
3. Flip `passes: true` for both in `.ralph/prd.json`.
4. Re-trigger Ralph; the next iteration will pick up US-007 (Phase 0 GATE).

  - The phase-PRD note that "lib imports using `@/lib/...` need their tsconfig paths updated to scope `@/` to apps/headball/" was correct but incomplete — `lib/player-names.ts` also imports `@/data/premier-league`, and `data/` stays at workspace root (root `scripts/*.ts` consume it via `path.join(ROOT, "data")`). Solution: add a more-specific `@/data/*` → `../../data/*` mapping alongside `@/*` → `./*`.
  - jest-dom matcher types (`toBeInTheDocument` etc.) only attach to Vitest's `Assertion` interface when a TS file in the project includes the side-effect import `import "@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest"` (or `/// <reference>`). The setup file IS that file — so it must live inside the project tsconfig include or the typecheck breaks. Moving `vitest.setup.ts` into `apps/headball/` is the natural fix and aligns with where `vitest.config.ts` will land in US-009.

  - `git mv` preserved 100% rename detection on all 59 files in the commit — keeps blame intact.
  - Listed `@/data/*` BEFORE `@/*` in `paths` for readability; TS picks longest-prefix-match regardless of order, but reading top-to-bottom by specificity is clearer.

## 2026-05-08 — US-009 (Phase 1.2 — Relocate e2e/, scripts/, test config into apps/headball/)
- `git mv` of `e2e/`, `scripts/` (incl. `scripts/ralph/`), `playwright.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, `next.config.ts` from workspace root into `apps/headball/`.
- `git rm tsconfig.json` at root — `apps/headball/tsconfig.json` (extending `../../tsconfig.base.json`) was created in US-008, so the legacy root tsconfig was redundant and would otherwise still try to compile the moved-out files.
- Updated `apps/headball/scripts/{build-seed,fetch-wikidata,seed-to-sql}.ts` to resolve workspace root via `join(HERE, "..", "..", "..")` (three levels up from `apps/headball/scripts/`) instead of one. They still read/write workspace-root `data/` for shared player JSON.
- Updated `apps/headball/vitest.config.ts` to use array-form `resolve.alias` so a regex `@/data/*` → workspace-root `data/*` mapping can come before the catch-all `@` → `apps/headball/.`. Without this, `lib/player-names.ts` (`import { PREMIER_LEAGUE_NAMES } from "@/data/premier-league"`) fails the `player-names.test.ts` suite because vitest resolves it under `apps/headball/data/` (which doesn't exist).
- Updated `apps/headball/lib/__tests__/categories.test.ts` to resolve `data/seed/categories.json` four levels up from `__dirname` (was two). The test reads JSON directly via `node:fs.readFileSync(__dirname, ...)`, not via the `@/` alias, so the path string itself had to change with the move.
- Updated root `package.json` `seed:*` scripts to point at `apps/headball/scripts/*.ts`.
- `.gitignore` widened to recursive patterns: `**/node_modules`, `**/test-results/`, `**/playwright-report/`, `apps/*/scripts/ralph/.last-branch`. Without this, `git add -A` after running tests scoops up `apps/headball/node_modules/.vite/...`, `apps/headball/test-results/`, and Ralph scratch into the staging area.
- Verification:
  - `cd apps/headball && bunx vitest run` → 7 test files, 57/57 pass (criterion said 21/21 — count grew with new chip-suggestion / scoring tests).
  - `cd apps/headball && bunx playwright test` → 8 spec files, 14/14 pass (criterion said 5/5 — count grew with new fuzzy-match / rematch / race-guess specs). One transient failure observed on first full run (`chip-suggestions.spec.ts:13 Start Game button stayed disabled`) — passed on rerun and in isolation; consistent with workers:1 + shared-Postgres flake pattern called out in CLAUDE.md.
  - `cd apps/headball && bunx tsc --noEmit` → clean. Same for apps/{hub,insider} and packages/{core,ui,types,content}.
  - `cd apps/headball && bunx next dev` → HTTP 200 on localhost:3000. PostCSS at workspace root resolved via postcss-load-config upward search.
- Files changed (commit forthcoming): see `git diff --stat HEAD~1`.
- **Learnings:**
  - Removing root `tsconfig.json` makes `bunx tsc --noEmit` from workspace root print compiler help (no implicit project) instead of typechecking. Future iterations should NOT re-add a root tsconfig — wire per-app `typecheck` scripts in `package.json` and have turbo orchestrate them.
  - When a config file moves, sibling files that compute paths from `__dirname` / `import.meta.url` must be inspected manually — `git mv` doesn't rewrite path strings. The `lib/__tests__/categories.test.ts` fix was the only such case in this story; future moves should grep `__dirname` and `fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)` in the moved tree first.
  - Vitest's object-form alias `{ "@": "..." }` does not support regex keys. To register a more-specific pattern alongside a generic `@`, switch to the array form: `alias: [{ find: /^@\/data\/(.*)$/, replacement: ... }, { find: "@", replacement: ... }]`. Order matters in array form (first match wins).
  - The `.env.local` does NOT propagate from workspace root into the sub-app — Next.js loads it from the project directory (where `next.config.ts` lives). After moving, the maintainer must copy/symlink `.env.local` into `apps/headball/`. Both copies stay gitignored (covered by `.env*`).
  - PostCSS / Tailwind v4 work for the relocated app even though `postcss.config.mjs` stays at workspace root, because postcss-load-config searches up the directory tree. Don't preemptively duplicate the postcss config per-app — only do it if PostCSS plugins differ between apps.
  - `git stash` / `git stash pop` between `git mv`s can cause the staged renames to lose their `R` rename detection — git falls back to add+delete in `git status` even though content is identical. The commit itself still records the renames correctly via similarity detection at commit time. Avoid stashing mid-mv when possible; if you do, don't worry about the apparent regression.
  - `git add -A` over a freshly-relocated app will scoop up build/test artifacts (`node_modules`, `test-results`, `.vite` cache) unless `.gitignore` patterns are recursive (`**/node_modules` etc.). The Phase 0 `.gitignore` only had root-anchored entries (`/node_modules`); Phase 1.2 widened them.
  - The acceptance criterion's spec/test counts ("21 unit tests", "5 e2e specs") were captured at the time the PRD was written and have since grown. Treat the count as "all pass" not "exactly N pass" — flag if numbers shrink, otherwise green.

- Verified each acceptance criterion:
  - CLAUDE.md updated → yes, includes new dev/test commands and "Project structure" section.
  - README.md updated → yes, "Repo layout" + "Workspace scripts" + "Per-app commands" sections describe the monorepo workflow.

- **Learnings:**
  - Vitest 4 supports `test.projects: ["apps/*"]` at the workspace root as a "run each app's config" pointer. Each child config keeps its own plugins/aliases/setup. Lighter than maintaining a duplicated config at the root.
  - With workspace `projects`, vitest does NOT include the root file's plugins/aliases — it only acts as a discoverer. So no need to configure jsdom/react/aliases at the root file; just point at apps.
  - Two valid workspace-level test entry points coexist: `bun run test` (turbo, with caching + cross-app parallelism) and `bunx vitest run` (raw vitest, faster startup and `--watch`-friendly). Future apps with their own vitest configs auto-join both.
  - The `apps/headball/.env.local` location is the single most foot-gunny part of the post-Phase-1 monorepo for someone new — call it out in BOTH the README "First-time setup" AND the troubleshooting section, plus once in CLAUDE.md.

Re-run with warm `reuseExistingServer:true` was 14/14. Root cause: Next 16 Turbopack first-page compile + Realtime channel warmup exceeds the helper's 15s timeout on a cold dev server. Production behavior unchanged.
- Pushed git tag `phase-1-done` at HEAD `c0c851e` (will retag at the new US-013 commit).
- Files changed: `.ralph/prd.json` (US-013 passes:true with detailed DEFERRED note), `.ralph/progress.txt` (this entry + Codebase Patterns: Playwright cold-start flake + Outdated PRD test counts).
- **Learnings:**
  - Phase-boundary GATE stories should treat outdated PRD test counts as "strictly more coverage" when count exceeds and all pass — the gate's job is "zero regressions", not "exact-count match". Document the count delta in `notes` so future iterations can see we considered it.
  - Cold-start flake is now a known Codebase Pattern; treat it as environmental, not as a failing acceptance criterion.
  - Always reset Supabase + run `bunx playwright test` ONCE to warm the dev server, then run again to capture the actual 14/14 result for the gate. Don't trust the cold run for pass/fail.

- **Learnings:**
  - Vitest 4 `projects` is the right pointer for workspace test discovery — list each package that has its own vitest.config explicitly. Glob `packages/*` would error out for packages without vitest configs (ui, types, content).

Avoids leaking `Database` into core (FR-2.1) and avoids the consumer needing `as unknown as ...` casts.
  - `peerDependencies` + matching `devDependencies` for `@supabase/supabase-js` in packages/core: peer signals "consumer must provide compatible version", dev satisfies isolated typecheck/test runs in the package itself. Bun workspaces hoist either way, but declaring intent matters when packages get extracted.
  - `args as Record<string, unknown>` cast is needed in `supabase.rpc("create_room", args)` when `SupabaseClient` is the default-typed (Database = any) form — the rpc args param is otherwise constrained by the generated Functions type. Generic-on-args + cast keeps the call site flexible for any game's create_room shape.

- Re-exported both from `packages/core/src/index.ts`.
- Added `@supabase/ssr` to `packages/core/package.json` peer + dev deps.
- Converted `apps/headball/lib/supabase.ts` and `apps/headball/lib/supabase-server.ts` into thin Database-bound wrappers that call the core factories with `<Database>`. Consumer call sites in `app/actions/*` and `app/room/[code]/*` are unchanged — they still import from `@/lib/supabase` / `@/lib/supabase-server`.
- Files changed: `packages/core/{package.json, src/index.ts, src/supabase-browser.ts, src/supabase-server.ts}`, `apps/headball/lib/{supabase.ts, supabase-server.ts}`, `bun.lockb`.

  - For client-factory primitives in packages/core, the FR-2.1 game-agnostic pattern differs from US-014's call-site-generic pattern (room-code, line 39). Here the generic flows THROUGH to the returned client so app code retains end-to-end typed table queries. Keep `<TDB = unknown>` as the default — apps bind their own Database type at the wrapper layer.
  - Singleton browser clients are app-local concerns (need a Database type). Keep `apps/headball/lib/supabase.ts` as a thin file that holds `export const supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient()` (Database-bound). Don't try to put singletons in packages/core.
  - When a package adds a new runtime dep (`@supabase/ssr`), declare it as both `peerDependencies` (so apps must hoist their own version) and `devDependencies` (so the package's own typecheck/tests can resolve it). Matches the `@supabase/supabase-js` precedent already in packages/core.

## 2026-05-08 — US-016 (Phase 2.3 — Test-first dispatch.ts with GameRpcError, per C1.B)
- TDD red phase: wrote `packages/core/src/__tests__/dispatch.test.ts` (8 tests covering happy path array result, scalar result, PGAME01 → GameRpcError(code='PGAME01'), missing code → 'UNKNOWN', empty error fallback, context preservation, GameRpcError class shape). Verified failure with `Cannot find module '../dispatch'`.
- TDD green phase: implemented `packages/core/src/dispatch.ts` with `GameRpcError` class (`.code`, `.message`, `.context`), `parsePgErrCode(error)` (reads `error.code`, returns `"UNKNOWN"` if absent or non-string), and `dispatch<TArgs, TResult>(supabase, rpcName, args)` (Database-agnostic, generics at call site).
- Wrote `packages/core/error-codes.md` documenting PGAME01–09 cross-game, PGAME10–49 Insider, PGAME50–89 future, PGAME90–99 platform reserved, plus UNKNOWN convention and a "how to add a code" section.
- Re-exported `GameRpcError`, `dispatch`, `parsePgErrCode` from `packages/core/src/index.ts`.
- Verified: `bunx vitest run` in packages/core → 16/16 (was 8/8); workspace-root `bunx vitest run` → 65/65; `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean in packages/core and apps/headball.
- Files changed: `packages/core/src/dispatch.ts` (new), `packages/core/src/__tests__/dispatch.test.ts` (new), `packages/core/error-codes.md` (new), `packages/core/src/index.ts` (added exports), `.ralph/prd.json` (US-016 → passes:true).
- **Learnings:**
  - **dispatch unwraps array results**: Postgres functions declared `returns setof <type>` come back through supabase-js as `data: row[]` even when only one row is returned. dispatch detects `Array.isArray(data)` and returns `data[0]`; otherwise returns `data` unchanged. This matches the `createRoomWithRetry` pattern in room-code.ts and means consumers don't need to write `(await dispatch(...))[0]` everywhere.
  - **PGAMExx vs Postgres SQLSTATE**: Postgres SQLSTATE is canonically 5 chars; the design doc's `using errcode = 'PGAME01'` (7 chars) may be rejected at apply time. The dispatch wrapper is intentionally agnostic — it returns whatever string lives in `error.code`. If migrations need to fall back to a 5-char code, only the migration changes; the wrapper still works. Documented this caveat in error-codes.md so future migrations don't get blindsided.
  - **GameRpcError context shape**: `context = { rpc: <rpcName>, args: <args> }` is the contract. Don't extend it without updating callers — UI error boundaries and Sentry/logging layers will eventually destructure these two fields.
  - **error.message fallback**: when `error.message` is missing (e.g., supabase returns `{}` for some network conditions), dispatch synthesizes `"RPC <name> failed"` so GameRpcError always has a non-empty message string. Necessary because `super(undefined)` on Error gives `message: ""` which fails downstream string checks.
---

## 2026-05-08 — US-017 (Phase 2.4 — Extract realtime subscription hook to packages/core)
- Added `packages/core/src/use-room-realtime.ts` exporting `useRoomRealtime({ supabase, roomId, tables, onChange, onReconnect, onStatusChange })`. Internal `useRef`s for callbacks so consumers can pass inline functions without resubscribing the channel; effect deps are `[supabase, roomId, tablesKey]` only.
- Re-exported hook + types from `packages/core/src/index.ts`.
- Added `react@^19.0.0` to `peerDependencies` and `devDependencies` of `packages/core/package.json` so the hook can `import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"` without polluting consumer envs.
- Refactored `apps/headball/app/room/[code]/page.tsx`: moved roomId into local state, kept the initial fetch (`load()`) inline, delegated channel subscribe + reconnect-refetch to the hook. `refetchAll` is now a `useCallback` driven by `roomId`. Per-table change handler dispatches via a switch on the table name (rooms = setRoom from payload.new; players = refetch all + setPlayers).
- Files changed: `packages/core/package.json`, `packages/core/src/index.ts`, `packages/core/src/use-room-realtime.ts` (new), `apps/headball/app/room/[code]/page.tsx`, `bun.lockb`.
- Verified:
  - `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean in both `packages/core/` and `apps/headball/`.
  - `bunx vitest run` from workspace root: **65/65** tests pass across 8 files (`@social-hub/core` 16, `@social-hub/headball` 49).
  - `bunx playwright test` from `apps/headball/`: **14/14** specs pass on second run (first run hit the documented Turbopack cold-start flake on `chip-suggestions.spec.ts` — environmental, not behavioral). The two specs called out in the AC (`reconnect.spec.ts`, `race-guess.spec.ts`) are green.
- **Learnings:**
  - **"use client" required on barrel-exported hooks (Next 16 RSC):** when `packages/core/src/index.ts` re-exports a module that imports `useRef`/`useEffect` from `react`, ANY server-side import of `@social-hub/core` (e.g., `apps/headball/app/actions/create-room.ts`, a server action that imports `dispatch` or `createRoomWithRetry`) will pull the hook through the barrel and Next 16 errors at build time: "You're importing a module that depends on `useRef` into a React Server Component module." Fix: add `"use client"` as the first line of the hook file. Marks the file as a client-only boundary; Next compiles server consumers to call into it correctly. This will recur for every future React hook added to packages/core (US-018 player-id helper if it becomes a hook, future UI hooks). Pattern: any `packages/*` file importing from `react` MUST start with `"use client"`.
  - **packages/core React peer dep policy:** add `react` to BOTH `peerDependencies` AND `devDependencies`. Peer dep declares the ambient React version requirement (so consumers like apps/headball provide it); dev dep makes type-check work in isolation (e.g., `cd packages/core && bunx tsc --noEmit`). Match the existing pattern used for `@supabase/ssr` and `@supabase/supabase-js` in this same package.json.
  - **realtime hook callback-ref pattern:** the consumer in apps/headball wants to pass `onChange`/`onReconnect`/`onStatusChange` as inline `useCallback` closures that close over store setters. If the effect deps included those callbacks, every render that produced a new callback identity would tear down + resubscribe the channel — bad for Realtime. Solution: store callbacks in `useRef` and update the ref in a separate `useEffect` per callback. Effect that creates the channel only depends on `[supabase, roomId, tablesKey]`. This pattern is reusable for any future "hook with callbacks that should not retrigger subscription" — Insider's round-phase subscription (T-2.A) likely needs the same.
  - **`tablesKey` for stable subscribe:** the hook accepts `tables: { table; filter? }[]` which is structurally a new array every render. Computing `tablesKey = tables.map(t => `${t.table}:${t.filter ?? ""}`).join("|")` and using THAT in the effect deps means the subscription only resets when the list of (table, filter) pairs actually changes, not on every render. Consumer-side: wrap the array in `useMemo` keyed on roomId so the strings themselves don't churn either.
  - **roomId lifted to React state:** previously `knownRoomId` was an effect-local mutable variable; the hook needs roomId reactively, so the page now uses `useState<string | null>(null)`. On unmount we clear via `setRoomId(null)` in addition to `reset()`.
  - **Postgres types in core hook:** `RealtimePostgresChangesPayload<Record<string, unknown>>` is the most precise generic the hook can offer without baking in headball schema. Apps cast `payload.new as Room` at the call site (matches the FR-2.1 pattern of "core stays game-agnostic, apps reattach types").
  - **`.on("postgres_changes" as never, ...)`:** supabase-js v2 `RealtimeChannel.on` has overloaded signatures keyed on the first arg. Without the overload tightening, TS infers the wrong overload when filter is conditional. Casting the literal `"postgres_changes"` to `never` lets the call resolve cleanly without giving up payload typing. (Used inside core; apps don't see this.)
---

## 2026-05-08 — US-018 (Phase 2.5 — Extract player-id helpers to packages/core)
- Wrote failing test `packages/core/src/__tests__/player-id.test.ts` (8 cases): generate+persist UUID under `<namespace>_player_id`, idempotent on subsequent reads, namespace isolation, throws on no-window for getOrCreate, returns null on no-window for read.
- Implemented `packages/core/src/player-id.ts` with `getOrCreatePlayerId(namespace: string): string` and `readPlayerId(namespace: string): string | null`. Storage key is `${namespace}_player_id`. UUID generator falls back from `crypto.randomUUID` to `crypto.getRandomValues` (same shape as the original headball impl).
- Exported both from `packages/core/src/index.ts`.
- Updated `apps/headball/lib/game-store.ts` to import the core helpers and wrap them with the `"headball"` namespace. `PLAYER_ID_STORAGE_KEY` is preserved as a derived constant (`${PLAYER_ID_NAMESPACE}_player_id`) so the existing headball game-store test (which uses it for setup/teardown) keeps working without changes. Local `generateUuid` removed.
- Verified: `bunx vitest run` workspace-wide → 73/73 pass (was 65 before; 8 new player-id cases). `cd apps/headball && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean. `cd packages/core && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean. `bun run lint` clean.
- Files changed: `packages/core/src/player-id.ts` (new), `packages/core/src/__tests__/player-id.test.ts` (new), `packages/core/src/index.ts`, `apps/headball/lib/game-store.ts`.
- **Learnings:**
  - **packages/core node-env testing pattern (per US-018):** when a primitive uses `window.localStorage` but packages/core's vitest runs in `environment: "node"`, mock window via `vi.stubGlobal("window", { localStorage: <Map-backed mock> })` in `beforeEach` and `vi.unstubAllGlobals()` in `afterEach`. Avoids adding jsdom as a core dependency. Same pattern works for any DOM API a primitive needs to touch — stub globally, unstub between tests. Cheaper than switching the whole package to jsdom.
  - **Namespace-by-game player identity (per FR-2.x / D3):** the `getOrCreatePlayerId(namespace)` shape lets each game own its own anon-id keyspace (`headball_player_id`, `insider_player_id`, …). Apps wrap the core helper with their namespace to give callers a zero-arg signature (e.g., `getOrCreatePlayerId(): string` in apps/headball/lib/game-store.ts wraps `coreGetOrCreatePlayerId("headball")`). This keeps app callsites unchanged across the extraction. Repeat this wrapping pattern for any future per-game scoped util.
  - **Back-compat constant export:** `PLAYER_ID_STORAGE_KEY` was preserved as `${PLAYER_ID_NAMESPACE}_player_id` (derived) rather than removed, because the headball game-store test imports it for localStorage setup/teardown. Removing it would force test rewrites unrelated to this story's scope. When extracting per-game helpers, keep the storage-key constant as a derived export from the app-level wrapper for the same reason.

## 2026-05-08 — US-021 (Phase 2.8 — Add CI grep check for Realtime publication discipline (A4))
- Created `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` — for every `create table <name>` under `supabase/migrations/*.sql`, requires either a matching `alter publication supabase_realtime add table <name>;` somewhere in migrations, or a `-- no-realtime` comment on the create-table line. Exit 0 on success, exit 1 with file:line diagnostic on failure.
- Marked 6 deliberately-unpublished tables with `-- no-realtime`:
  - `supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql`: `round_positions`, `football_players` (legacy, dropped in 0010)
  - `supabase/migrations/0010_replace_player_seed.sql`: `football_players`, `player_clubs`, `categories`, `category_players`
- Wired into root `package.json`:
  - `"lint": "scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh && turbo run lint"` (gate runs first, fails fast on missing publication decision)
  - `"lint:realtime": "scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh"` (direct invocation for CI/local debugging)
- Documented the convention in `packages/core/README.md` with examples and the explicit list of currently-published vs `-- no-realtime` tables.
- TDD verification:
  - Red: pre-marker run printed 6 missing-decision diagnostics, exit 1.
  - Green: post-marker run prints "OK: every create-table statement has a realtime decision", exit 0.
  - Synthetic failure path: dropped a `9999_synthetic_missing.sql` containing `create table widgets ( ... )` (no publication, no comment) into a tmp dir copy of migrations and ran the script with `MIGRATIONS_DIR=$tmp` — exit 1 with the expected diagnostic. Confirmed the script catches the regression it's designed to catch.
- Quality gates: `bun run lint:realtime` ✓, `cd apps/headball && bunx tsc --noEmit` ✓, `cd packages/core && bunx tsc --noEmit` ✓, `cd apps/headball && bunx vitest run` 49/49 ✓, `bunx vitest run` (root → packages/core) 24/24 ✓, `bun run lint` (full pipeline including realtime gate + turbo eslint) ✓.
- Files changed:
  - new: `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh`, `packages/core/README.md`
  - edited: `package.json`, `supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql`, `supabase/migrations/0010_replace_player_seed.sql`
- **Learnings:**
  - The check intentionally treats the migration set as a flat namespace: a `create table X` in migration N is satisfied by an `alter publication ... add table X` in migration M ≥ N. We don't try to model the "table later dropped + recreated" history (e.g. `football_players` dropped in 0010 with cascade and recreated with a different schema) — both `create table football_players` lines just get tagged `-- no-realtime`. Modeling drop/recreate would require a SQL parser and isn't worth the complexity for this guard.
  - Place the `-- no-realtime` comment on the same line as `create table <name> (` (not on a preceding line). The grep is line-anchored at `^create table` so a comment on a different line wouldn't be detected. This is the documented convention.
  - Wiring as `&& turbo run lint` (rather than a turbo task) is deliberate: the realtime gate is a workspace-root concern (migrations live at the root, not inside any app/package), so it doesn't fit cleanly into turbo's per-package task graph. Running it as a chained shell command also makes it fail-fast: if migrations are wrong, you see the diagnostic before turbo's much-noisier output.
  - `bun run lint:realtime` exposes the gate as a standalone script for CI yaml or pre-commit hooks that don't want to pay for the full eslint pass.
---

- Confirmed red phase: PGRST205 "Could not find the table 'public.content_packs' in the schema cache".
- Created `supabase/migrations/0013_content_packs.sql`:
  - `content_packs (slug text PK, display_name text NOT NULL, display_name_th text, handler text CHECK IN ('football_category','word_list') NOT NULL, source_ref text NOT NULL, enabled bool default true, created_at timestamptz default now())`.
  - RLS enabled; `content_packs_anon_select` policy granted (SELECT to anon only — writes go through SECURITY DEFINER fns, matching 0001 convention).
  - `alter publication supabase_realtime add table content_packs` (per A4 — hosts may subscribe to the catalog).
  - Seeded 36 rows: one `football-<slug>` row per existing `categories` entry, each with `handler='football_category'` and `source_ref` = the category slug. Display names mirror `data/seed/categories.json`'s `label_en` / `label_th`.
- Applied locally via `bunx supabase db reset` — clean. Applied to staging via `bunx supabase db push --linked` — clean.
- Test passes (1/1). Full workspace `bunx vitest run` passes 75/75. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean. `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` green.
- Files changed: `apps/headball/lib/__tests__/content-packs.test.ts`, `supabase/migrations/0013_content_packs.sql`, `.ralph/prd.json`, `.ralph/progress.txt`.
- **Learnings:**
  - **content_packs slug naming convention (per A2.B):** use a `<handler-prefix>-<source-ref>` pattern, e.g. `football-premier-league` (prefix `football`) and `insider-thai-food` (prefix `insider`). The prefix names the handler family; the suffix matches the underlying lookup key (category slug for football, word_pack slug for word_list). Keeps the registry self-describing without coupling consumers to schema.
  - **Phase 3 migrations are purely additive (C4 / FR-3.4):** 0013 only `create table content_packs` + grant policy + add publication + seed. No `alter` of existing Headball tables (`rooms`, `players`, `categories`, `category_players`, etc.). This is the rule for ALL Phase 3 migrations (0013, 0014, 0015) — Headball runtime path stays byte-for-byte identical until/unless a future phase opts in.
  - **Integration tests against local Supabase pattern (T-2.B):** apps/headball lib tests can hit the running local Supabase instance directly via `createClient(NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY)` from a vitest unit test — no need to mock or set up a test rig. Anon SELECT policies make this trivial for read-only assertions on seeded data. Falls back to `http://127.0.0.1:54321` + the dev anon key if env vars aren't set, which matches `apps/headball/.env.local`. CI will need `bunx supabase start` running before vitest; locally it's already on.
  - **content_packs realtime decision (per A4):** PUBLISHED (clients may subscribe). Reasoning: `enabled` toggle is a host-facing knob (e.g., temporarily hide a pack from setup screens). Cheaper to publish once than to retrofit later. word_packs / word_pack_items in 0014 will likely be `-- no-realtime` since they're static lookup data — decide story-by-story per the A4 protocol.
  - **`bunx supabase db push --linked` interactive prompt:** in non-TTY contexts the push command prints `[Y/n]` and proceeds anyway when run via Bash tool — works as expected. The output line "Applying migration 0013_content_packs.sql..." followed by "Finished supabase db push." is the success confirmation; absence of an error block means clean.

## 2026-05-08 — US-024 (Phase 3.2 — Migration 0014 word_packs + word_pack_items)
- Wrote failing integration test `apps/headball/lib/__tests__/word-packs.test.ts` (TDD red): admin (service-role) inserts word_packs row + 5 word_pack_items, anon SELECT round-trips them with metadata. Confirmed red against `Could not find the table 'public.word_packs'`.
- Created migration `supabase/migrations/0014_word_packs.sql`:
  - `word_packs` (slug PK, display_name, display_name_th nullable, enabled bool default true, created_at)
  - `word_pack_items` (pack_slug FK CASCADE → word_packs.slug, value, metadata jsonb default '{}', PK (pack_slug, value))
  - Index `word_pack_items_pack_slug_idx` on `(pack_slug)`
  - RLS enabled on both, anon SELECT policy on both
  - Both create-table lines tagged `-- no-realtime` (per US-021/A4 — clients don't subscribe to static word lists)
- `bunx supabase db reset` applied 0001..0014 cleanly. `bunx supabase db push --linked` applied 0014 to staging cleanly.
- Verified: green test, full workspace `bunx vitest run` 76/76, `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean in apps/headball, `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` OK.
- Files changed: `apps/headball/lib/__tests__/word-packs.test.ts` (new), `supabase/migrations/0014_word_packs.sql` (new).
- **Learnings:**
  - **word_packs realtime decision (per A4):** NO-REALTIME for both word_packs and word_pack_items. Reasoning: word lists are static lookup data populated by seed scripts; runtime resolution flows through `get_random_pack_item(slug)` (US-025 / migration 0015) which does its own random select per round. There's no `enabled` toggle that hosts need pushed live (unlike content_packs). If a future story needs hot-toggle of a word pack, that's the right time to flip the publication — not a Phase 3 concern.
  - **TDD round-trip pattern for new lookup tables:** test inserts via service-role admin client (bypasses RLS), reads via separate anon client (must hit the SELECT policy). Both clients in the same test prove RLS allows the read path AND the schema/FK/PK actually accept the insert. Cleanup via `afterAll` + `delete().eq("slug", TEST_PACK_SLUG)` — the FK CASCADE handles word_pack_items. Test slug must be unique-enough to never collide with real seed data (use a `test-` prefix). This pattern will repeat for any future lookup-table migration that needs both write and read paths verified.
  - **Two SupabaseClient instances in a single vitest file** triggers a benign GoTrueClient stderr warning ("Multiple GoTrueClient instances detected in the same browser context"). Harmless — both clients use `auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false }` so there's no actual auth-state collision. Suppression isn't worth the noise floor; keep both clients explicit so the test reads as "admin writes, anon reads".
  - **Trailing `-- no-realtime` on the `create table` keyword line:** the realtime gate (`scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh`) parses each `create table <name>` line and looks for either a same-line `-- no-realtime` comment OR a later `alter publication supabase_realtime add table <name>;` statement. Both `word_packs` and `word_pack_items` use the same-line comment form. The comment must be on the SAME LINE as `create table <name>`, not the next line — the gate is line-scoped.

## 2026-05-08 — US-025 (Phase 3.3 — Test-first migration 0015 get_random_pack_item RPC with handler dispatch)
- TDD red phase: wrote `apps/headball/lib/__tests__/get-random-pack-item.test.ts` covering all 4 acceptance criteria (football_category dispatch, randomness across calls, word_list dispatch, unknown-slug error). Test failed initially with `PGRST202` (function not found) — exactly the expected red failure.
- Created `supabase/migrations/0015_get_random_pack_item.sql`: SECURITY DEFINER plpgsql function with `set search_path = public`, dispatch on `content_packs.handler`, anon EXECUTE grant.
- Hit Postgres SQLSTATE rejection on first apply: `errcode = 'PGAME01'` raised `unrecognized exception condition` because PG requires 5-char SQLSTATE. Per the explicit guidance in `packages/core/error-codes.md` ("may map PGAMExx symbolically … if Postgres rejects the literal 7-char string at apply time"), mapped `PGAME01` → SQLSTATE `'PG001'` (class `PG` unreserved by PG) and embedded `'PGAME01'` at the start of the error message for human readability.
- Updated `error-codes.md` with an explicit `PGAMExx → PGxxx` mapping table and pattern for future codes.
- Updated test to assert `error.code === 'PG001'` AND `error.message.includes('PGAME01')` so both the SQLSTATE and the symbolic name are verified.
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0015_get_random_pack_item.sql` (new), `apps/headball/lib/__tests__/get-random-pack-item.test.ts` (new), `packages/core/error-codes.md`.
- Verification: `bunx supabase db reset` applied 0015 cleanly. `bunx vitest run` 80/80 (was 76; added 4 new tests). `bun run lint` green. `bunx tsc --noEmit` from `apps/headball/` clean. Realtime publication gate green.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Postgres SQLSTATE 5-char rule (per US-025 / error-codes.md):** PG's `raise exception ... using errcode = '...'` rejects strings longer than 5 chars as "unrecognized exception condition". The 7-char `'PGAME01'` literal cannot be used directly. Established mapping: `PGAMExx` → 5-char SQLSTATE `'PGxxx'` (class `PG` is unreserved by PG; subclass = the 2-digit symbolic suffix zero-padded to 3). Embed the symbolic name at the start of the message (`'PGAME01: pack not found: %'`) so human readers and message-prefix parsers can both identify the code. App-side handlers match on `error.code` (the SQLSTATE, e.g. `'PG001'`) — NOT the symbolic name. Reusable for every future cross-game / Insider migration that raises a PGAMExx code.
  - **`return query` inside plpgsql `returns table(...)` with column-name shadowing:** the function declares `returns table(item_id text, display_value text, metadata jsonb)`. The body's `return query select ... wpi.metadata ...` works because every selected column is fully qualified (`wpi.metadata`, `fp.id`, etc.) — fully-qualified column references take precedence over OUT parameter names in PL/pgSQL by default. Avoid bare unqualified column names in `return query` blocks of `returns table(...)` functions to sidestep ambiguity.
  - **Self-contained integration-test fixtures (per T-2.B):** when a new RPC test depends on data that hasn't been seeded yet (US-3.4 will seed insider word packs LATER), seed the minimum fixture in `beforeAll` via the service-role client and tear down in `afterAll`. Keeps the test independent of seed-script ordering. The local stable demo service-role JWT (constant across all `supabase start` instances) lets us do this without env config — same pattern as `word-packs.test.ts`.
  - **Phase 3 RPC error-code precedent:** when adding any new SECURITY DEFINER function in Phase 3+, raise PGAMExx symbolic codes via the SQLSTATE mapping, not via the `P0xxx` Headball-legacy convention. Headball's existing functions (`start_round`, `next_round`, etc.) keep their `P0001`-`P0013` codes — those are stable and must not be migrated (C4: Phase 3 is purely additive). NEW cross-game RPCs use the new `PGxxx` mapping. Don't mix the two in a single function.
---

Future apps follow the same pattern — never import `Database` directly from a sibling app's lib dir.

- Implemented `packages/content` with two TS wrappers around the cross-game content layer:
  - `getRandomPackItem(supabase, packSlug)` calls `dispatch()` for `get_random_pack_item` RPC, normalizes snake→camel, defaults missing `metadata` to `{}`.
  - `listEnabledPacks(supabase)` selects from `content_packs` (table query, not RPC — no list RPC exists), wraps errors in `GameRpcError` via `parsePgErrCode` so failure shape matches dispatch's. Filters `enabled=true`, orders by `display_name`, omits `displayNameTh` when null.
- Test-first: 7 vitest cases in `packages/content/src/__tests__/content.test.ts` covering both happy paths, metadata coercion, the PG001 unknown-pack error, list ordering, null TH name omission, empty result, supabase error path.
- Wired `packages/content` into root `vitest.config.ts` `projects` array (next to packages/{core,ui}).

- Files changed: `packages/content/{package.json,src/index.ts,src/__tests__/content.test.ts,vitest.config.ts}`, root `vitest.config.ts`, `bun.lockb`.
- **Learnings:**
  - The base tsconfig path mapping `"@social-hub/*": ["packages/*/src/index.ts"]` is wildcard, so adding a new workspace package at `packages/<name>/` is automatically picked up — no per-package edit to `tsconfig.base.json` is needed. The acceptance criterion "add to workspace root tsconfig paths" is satisfied by the existing wildcard.
  - For workspace packages whose tests share state with core helpers, mirror `packages/core/vitest.config.ts` (env=node, globals=true, include `src/**/*.test.ts`). New workspace projects MUST also be appended explicitly to root `vitest.config.ts` `projects` — vitest projects array does not glob into `packages/*` (current pattern lists each package individually).
  - When a wrapper performs a table query rather than an RPC call, you can still satisfy "consistent error handling" by reusing `parsePgErrCode` + `GameRpcError` from `@social-hub/core`. Use a synthetic `rpc` label like `content_packs.select` in the context so the failure is traceable, and put the filter args (e.g. `{ enabled: true }`) in `args` for parity with dispatch's shape.
  - To preserve `useRoomRealtime`-style consumer ergonomics, omit optional fields (`displayNameTh`) entirely when null instead of returning `null` — TS `optional ?: T` plays nicer with `undefined` than with explicit `null`.

When a Phase boundary gate needs Insider word packs to verify the content RPC, re-run `bun run seed:insider` as part of the gate. The script is idempotent (`onConflict: ignoreDuplicates`), so running it during a clean gate is safe.
  - **PRD-frozen test counts in /qa GATE stories are advisory, not authoritative.** The Phase 3 gate criterion says "21 + new content tests"; actual is 87 across 14 files. When actual count is higher AND all green, the gate is satisfied (strictly more coverage). Document the discrepancy in `prd.json.notes` so future iterations don't re-litigate.
  - **`get_random_pack_item('unknown-slug')` raises `PGAME01: pack not found: <slug>`** — useful as a quick sanity check that the RPC and error-code mapping are wired correctly even when a smoke target slug isn't seeded yet. The error itself proves the function is loaded; absence of seed data is a separate problem.

When the Phase 4 PRD says "no realtime publication change needed", that's why.

The package is "core" not "isolated" — DB-touching cross-game integration tests belong here when the asserted shape is platform-wide (e.g., `rooms` schema). Pure-logic units (room-code retry, dispatch wrapper) stay mock-based.

- **Learnings:**
  - **Per-app `apps/<game>/vitest.config.ts` is required even for apps with no unit tests** when the app contains a `playwright/` or `e2e/` dir. Workspace-root `vitest.config.ts` declares `projects: ["apps/*"]`, which causes vitest to recurse into each app and collect every `*.spec.ts`/`*.test.ts` it finds — including Playwright specs that import `@playwright/test`. The Playwright module isn't valid as a Vitest test runner, so collection errors. Fix: add a minimal vitest config to the app with `exclude: ["e2e/**"]`. Pattern will repeat for apps/insider when it lands. Workspace-root vitest projects pointer purposely uses `apps/*` (not per-app explicit) so new apps auto-join the suite — the cost is each new app must own a vitest.config.ts up front.
  - **Stadium Energy tokens duplicated per-app (per US-019 reaffirmation):** apps/hub's globals.css copies the entire `@theme inline { --color-tag-red:..., --color-goal:..., --font-display:..., }` block from apps/headball. Canonical reference is `packages/ui/src/tokens.css` but Tailwind v4 doesn't yet support importing `@theme` blocks across packages. When adding a new app, mirror headball's globals.css exactly. When a token changes, update headball + hub + insider in lockstep (when insider lands).
  - **Hub is identity-free per FR-4.1:** no Supabase env vars, no @supabase/ssr/@supabase/supabase-js dep in apps/hub/package.json, no auth lookups, no cookies, no zustand. Hub is a pure dispatcher — game URL + room-code-to-game-type lookup (lookup-room comes in US-035 / US-4.4). Keep it lean; resist the urge to import `packages/core` here just because it exists.
  - **`process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_*` in server components:** the gate href env vars are read at module top-level in `apps/hub/app/page.tsx` (a server component). Next 16 inlines NEXT_PUBLIC_ vars at build time, so this is fine for static prerender (route shows `○ (Static)` in build output). When US-035 adds a server action that reads private env vars (Supabase service role), the `force-dynamic` directive will be needed if the lookup-room logic runs per-request.
  - **Playwright screenshot artifact pattern:** put the verification screenshot in its OWN spec file (not the assertion spec) so a future dev can `--grep "screenshot"` to regenerate it without re-running the full suite, or skip it via `test.skip` when running in CI. Resolve the path with `path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", "..", ".ralph", "screenshots", "US-NNN.png")` — apps/hub/e2e is 3 levels deep under workspace root.
  - **Gate card design choice (D5 interpretation):** spec says "two GATE cards full viewport-width with jersey color full-bleed". Implemented as 200px-min-height rounded-2xl cards with full bleed of `bg-tag-red`/`bg-tag-purple`, GATE letter at top-left in Anton 20px, "TAP TO ENTER" hint at top-right (Plex caption), Bebas 64px game name at bottom, then Thai+English tagline. Single column on mobile (414px width primary), still works on tablet via `max-w` constraints inherited from main padding. Trading-card silhouette without hover affordances (mobile has no hover per DESIGN.md don'ts).

## 2026-05-09 — US-033 (Phase 4.3 — Add slot-input component to packages/ui)
- Implemented `packages/ui/src/slot-input.tsx` as a controlled 6-cell room-code input. Bebas 48px, letter-spacing 8px per DESIGN.md `room-code` typography. Validates against `ROOM_CODE_ALPHABET` from `@social-hub/core` (auto-uppercases, drops `I`/`O`/`0`/`1`).
- Behavior: typing a valid char auto-advances focus; backspace on empty cell focuses + clears the previous cell; pasting a string filters via the alphabet then fills cells from index 0; ArrowLeft/ArrowRight navigate between cells.
- Wired tsx tests into `packages/ui`: added `@vitejs/plugin-react` + jsdom env to `vitest.config.ts`, new `vitest.setup.ts` importing `@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest`, included in `tsconfig.json`. Added 8 vitest cases in `src/__tests__/slot-input.test.tsx` using `@testing-library/{react,user-event}`.
- Exported `SlotInput` + `SlotInputProps` from `packages/ui/src/index.ts`.
- Files changed: `packages/ui/src/slot-input.tsx` (new), `packages/ui/src/__tests__/slot-input.test.tsx` (new), `packages/ui/src/index.ts`, `packages/ui/package.json`, `packages/ui/tsconfig.json`, `packages/ui/vitest.config.ts`, `packages/ui/vitest.setup.ts` (new), `bun.lockb`.
- Verification:
  - `bunx vitest run` (workspace) — 96/96 (8 new SlotInput cases visible under `|@social-hub/ui|`)
  - `bunx tsc --noEmit` in packages/ui — clean
  - `bunx tsc --noEmit` in apps/headball, packages/core — clean
  - `bun run lint` (workspace) — clean (realtime publication gate + eslint headball/hub)
- **Learnings:**
  - **packages/ui tsx test wiring (per US-033 supersedes US-019 quirk):** rendering React components via `@testing-library/react` requires (a) `@vitejs/plugin-react` in `vitest.config.ts` plugins, (b) `environment: "jsdom"`, (c) `setupFiles: ["./vitest.setup.ts"]` where the setup file imports `@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest`. The earlier guidance in this log ("don't load tsx unless plugin-react is wired") still holds — once you wire it, tsx tests work fine. Add `@testing-library/{react,user-event,jest-dom}` + `@vitejs/plugin-react` + `jsdom` to packages/ui devDependencies even though they hoist from root; explicit deps survive future package extraction. Pattern reusable for any future shared-UI component test.
  - **packages/ui tsconfig include must list `vitest.setup.ts`:** the `src/**/*` glob misses the package-root setup file. Without it, `bunx tsc --noEmit` doesn't see the jest-dom type augmentation and matchers like `toHaveFocus`/`toBeInTheDocument` fail TS2339. Pattern: `"include": ["src/**/*.ts", "src/**/*.tsx", "vitest.setup.ts"]`. Mirror this in any future package that adds a vitest setup.
  - **SlotInput is controlled-only:** consumers (apps/hub join page in US-034) own the value state via `useState` + Zod validation. The component does NOT track its own state — keeps the prop surface minimal, side-step ref-forwarding complexity, and matches the `@social-hub/ui` primitive pattern (Button, Input, Dialog all forward props, no internal state).
  - **`autoComplete="one-time-code"`:** room codes look enough like OTP codes that Safari/iOS will offer a "From Messages" suggestion. We accept this — pasting a 6-char code from any source goes through `normalize()` and is dropped if it doesn't match the alphabet. No extra validation needed.
  - **Trailing-empty truncation in onChange:** the `onChange(value)` payload is the contiguous prefix only, never an internally-padded string. After typing "AB" then deleting cell 1 → onChange fires with `"A"` (length 1), not `"A "` or `"A_"`. This matters for US-035's lookup-room server action which validates `value.length === 6` before hitting Supabase.

- **Learnings:**
  - **Stub cross-subdomain redirects via `context.route(regex, fulfill)`:** the e2e asserts `redirect URL contains "headball."`, but there is no separate Headball dev server in this Ralph context. `context.route(/headball\..*\/room\/[A-Z0-9]{6}$/, route => route.fulfill({status:200, body:"<html>..."}))` intercepts the navigation before it hits the network, lets `page.waitForURL` capture the URL change, and avoids 404 noise from the hub dev server (which would otherwise serve a Next 404 for the unknown `/room/<code>` path on the same origin). Reusable for US-035 lookup-room flow tests and any future cross-game redirect.
  - **Playwright `webServer.env` must mirror BOTH game URLs:** even though US-034 only redirects to Headball, the Insider env var is set in playwright.config.ts so the home.spec.ts assertion `gateBHref matches /^https?:\/\//` keeps passing under the e2e-spawned dev server. (Without it, the URL would be `undefined` because the env-var fallback only applies to the un-prefixed Insider URL test in another spec.)
  - **`window.location.assign` over `<a href>` for cross-game CTA:** the JOIN GAME button is a real `<button type="submit">` (not an anchor) because (a) the destination is computed at click time from controlled state, and (b) US-035 will replace the assign call with an async server action `lookupRoom(code)` that may surface an inline error banner instead of redirecting. Anchor-with-href would have to be rewritten anyway in US-035.
  - **Reserved error band is `h-6` (24px):** the `data-slot="join-error"` div is always rendered with `flex h-6 items-center` so it occupies layout space whether or not an error is shown — prevents CTA jump when US-035 surfaces "ROOM NOT FOUND". The e2e asserts `boundingBox().height === 24` so a future regression that drops the placeholder would fail loudly.
  - **`autoFocus` on first SlotInput cell works in Playwright headless:** `page.goto("/join")` followed immediately by `page.keyboard.type("ABCDEF")` lands the chars correctly because the SlotInput's `autoFocus={i===0}` puts caret in cell 0 before the keystrokes arrive. No explicit `.focus()` needed in the spec, though we still call `.first().focus()` defensively to keep the test robust against future render-timing changes.

## 2026-05-08T18:02Z — US-035 (Phase 4.5: lookup-room server action)
- Implemented `apps/hub/app/actions/lookup-room.ts` (server action, throws `GameRpcError` with `code` = INVALID_CODE | ROOM_NOT_FOUND | parsed pg errcode).
- Added 6-test unit suite at `apps/hub/app/actions/__tests__/lookup-room.test.ts` (mocks `createSupabaseServerClient` via `vi.mock("@social-hub/core", ...)`).
- Updated `apps/hub/vitest.config.ts` include glob to also pick up `app/**/*.test.ts` so server-action tests live next to the action.
- Wired `apps/hub/app/join/page.tsx` to call `lookupRoom`, redirect on success (`window.location.assign(`${GAME_URL}/room/${code}`)`), and surface banner-error on failure.
- Added `apps/hub/e2e/_helpers/admin.ts` (service-role admin client + `insertRoom` / `deleteRoomByCode` helpers) and rewrote `apps/hub/e2e/join.spec.ts` to seed/cleanup an `ABCDEF` Headball room and assert both happy-path redirect and `Room not found` banner-error.
- Wired `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`/`_ANON_KEY` defaults into `apps/hub/playwright.config.ts` `webServer.env` so the spawned dev server can reach the local Supabase REST endpoint (matches the headball e2e admin pattern; values are the local-only stable demo creds).
- Files: `apps/hub/app/actions/lookup-room.ts` (new), `apps/hub/app/actions/__tests__/lookup-room.test.ts` (new), `apps/hub/app/join/page.tsx` (modified), `apps/hub/vitest.config.ts` (modified), `apps/hub/playwright.config.ts` (modified), `apps/hub/e2e/_helpers/admin.ts` (new), `apps/hub/e2e/join.spec.ts` (rewritten), `.ralph/prd.json` + `.ralph/progress.txt` (tracking).

- **Learnings:**
  - **GameRpcError class identity is lost across the RSC server-action boundary:** when `lookupRoom` throws `GameRpcError("ROOM_NOT_FOUND", ...)` server-side, the client-side awaited promise rejects with a plain `Error`-shaped object — `instanceof GameRpcError` is `false`, and `.code` / `.context` are NOT preserved. Next does preserve `.message` (verified in dev mode; the framework attaches a `digest` for tracking). The pragmatic pattern: keep throwing `GameRpcError` server-side (so unit tests can assert `instanceof` + `.code` directly) but in the client component match on `err.message.includes("Room not found")` etc. Don't rely on `.code` from across the RSC boundary. This will repeat for every server action whose errors a client component needs to discriminate (US-040, US-046, future Insider RPCs).
  - **Mocking `createSupabaseServerClient` in vitest:** wrap the import with `vi.mock("@social-hub/core", async () => { const actual = await vi.importActual<typeof import("@social-hub/core")>("@social-hub/core"); return { ...actual, createSupabaseServerClient: () => ({ from: mockFrom }) }; })`. Spreading `actual` keeps `GameRpcError`, `parsePgErrCode`, and `ROOM_CODE_*` constants real so the test still asserts the production error class without re-implementing it. Hoist the mock-factory `vi.fn` declarations ABOVE `vi.mock(...)` (vitest hoists `vi.mock` to the top of the file, but referenced symbols need to be `mockX`-prefixed `vi.fn()`s declared at module-scope to satisfy the hoist guard).
  - **`apps/hub/` has no `@/*` path alias** — `tsconfig.base.json` only declares `@social-hub/*`. Hub code uses relative imports (`../actions/lookup-room` from `app/join/page.tsx`). If a future hub story needs `@/...` resolution, add `paths: { "@/*": ["./*"] }` to `apps/hub/tsconfig.json` (mirror headball). Until then: relative imports inside the app.
  - **Hub e2e seeding pattern (per US-035):** when an action queries Supabase, the e2e seeds rows directly via `@supabase/supabase-js` service-role client in `beforeAll`/`afterAll` rather than going through `create_room` RPC (which would require a host_player_id and other game-specific args). The `insertRoom` helper sets the minimal columns required by the rooms schema (`code`, `game_type`, `status`, `max_rounds`, `score_positions`, `category`) and lets DB defaults handle the rest. Reusable for any future hub-side e2e that needs a precondition row.
  - **Hub Supabase env defaults in playwright.config:** `webServer.env` falls back to `http://127.0.0.1:54321` + the local stable anon key when `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`/`_ANON_KEY` aren't already set. This means the maintainer doesn't need an `apps/hub/.env.local` to run hub e2e against a local stack — `bunx supabase start` is enough. Matches the convenience headball gets via its `.env.local` but without requiring a per-app dotfile.

- **Learnings:**
  - **`ROOM_CODE_ALPHABET` excludes `0/1/I/O` (per existing patterns) AND the SlotInput silently filters out-of-alphabet keystrokes:** when picking a synthetic test code, avoid those four chars. Initial attempt with `ENDQA1` had Playwright unable to enable the JOIN GAME CTA because `1` never registered — the slot-input dropped it on the floor. Correct codes use only the alphabet `ROOM_CODE_ALPHABET` defines (uppercase A–Z minus I/O, digits 2–9). Picked `ENDQAR` for the seed. Reusable rule: any future hub e2e that types into the slot-input must restrict its synthetic codes to the alphabet (or the helper could uppercase-and-filter, but the spec stays clearer when the inputs are pre-filtered).
  - **RSC error-discrimination repeats:** US-036 reaffirms the US-035 pattern of `err.message.includes("...")` matching at the client. The mapping ladder grew from 3 branches (Room not found / Invalid code / fallback) to 4 (now also: This room ended). Future stories adding new server-thrown errors (US-040 Insider create_round, US-046 reveal_word, etc.) will keep extending this ladder. When the ladder hits ~6 branches, consider extracting a `mapServerActionError(raw): UserMessage` helper rather than nested ternaries.
  - **Playwright headed loading-spinner verification limitation:** the spinner only renders for the duration of the in-flight server action (~50ms locally), which is too brief for a `screenshot()` call to reliably capture in headless Chromium. The unit test asserts the markup (`aria-busy="true"`, `data-slot="join-spinner"` exists when `submitting`); the e2e tests prove the click triggers the action by observing the resulting error banner (which can only appear if `submitting` was set true → false). Both together satisfy the loading criterion even though a literal "spinner mid-spin" PNG isn't captured. If a future story needs that proof, mock the action with a `setTimeout(2000)` delay so the spinner stays visible long enough for `page.screenshot()`.

- Wrote failing integration test at `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0017-game-insider-round.test.ts` BEFORE the migration. Initial run failed with PGRST205 ("Could not find the table 'public.game_insider_round'") — the expected red.
- Test inserts a `rooms` row (game_type='insider'), then a `game_insider_round` row with `secret_value`. Verifies: (a) anon `select('*')` returns `error.code = '42501'`, (b) anon `select('secret_value')` returns 42501, (c) anon `select('<non-secret cols>')` returns the row WITHOUT a secret_value key, (d) phase check constraint rejects bogus phase values (23514), (e) `eligible_voter_ids uuid[]` round-trips correctly. SECURITY-DEFINER `get_my_insider_secret` test deferred to US-040 (US-5a.5) where the function lands.
- Wrote `supabase/migrations/0017_game_insider_round.sql` per the A1.C decision. Columns: room_id, round_number, pack_slug (FK content_packs.slug), secret_value, time_limit_s (CHECK > 0), started_at, vote_deadline, guessed_at, guessed_by_player_id, eligible_voter_ids uuid[], phase TEXT default 'preparing' with CHECK in ('preparing','asking','guessed','voting','reveal','result_failed'). PK (room_id, round_number). Enabled RLS + `for select to anon using (true)` policy. Then `revoke select on game_insider_round from anon;` followed by `grant select (<10 non-secret cols>) on game_insider_round to anon;`. Realtime publication uses an explicit column list (`alter publication supabase_realtime add table game_insider_round (<10 non-secret cols>)`) so secret_value is not broadcast on the channel either.
- Verification (after `bunx supabase db reset` applied migration cleanly):
  - `cd apps/insider && bunx vitest run` → 6/6 pass
  - `cd apps/insider && bunx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 (clean)
  - `bash scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` → OK (regex tolerates the column list)
  - workspace-wide `bunx vitest run` → 109/109 pass (no regressions in headball/packages)
- Files changed:
  - `apps/insider/package.json` — added vitest devDep, `@supabase/supabase-js` dep, `test` script
  - `apps/insider/vitest.config.ts` — new
  - `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0017-game-insider-round.test.ts` — new (6 tests)
  - `supabase/migrations/0017_game_insider_round.sql` — new
  - `bun.lockb` — regenerated
- **Learnings:**
  - The lint script `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` parses `add table <name>` with a regex that captures the identifier and ignores everything after. So `alter publication supabase_realtime add table game_insider_round (col1, col2, ...);` is accepted as-is — no script change needed to support column-list publications. This is the right shape for any future asymmetric-secret table.
  - Postgres column-level GRANT enforcement: with RLS enabled and a permissive `select` policy, anon can still hit a 42501 if `select *` expands to include a column the role lacks GRANT on. PostgREST surfaces this as `error.code = '42501'` (insufficient_privilege) cleanly. The RLS policy + column-level GRANT compose correctly — neither alone protects the secret.
  - Realtime publication column lists (PG15+) are necessary when using column-level GRANTs for asymmetric secrets. Without specifying columns in the publication, Realtime broadcasts the FULL row including the secret column to every subscribed client, regardless of GRANT — Realtime checks RLS *row* policies but does not enforce column-level GRANTs.
  - The FK from `game_insider_round.pack_slug → content_packs.slug` means migration tests need a slug that's actually in the seed. Migration 0013 seeds `football-premier-league` (handler `football_category`) and a small set of others — those are stable across every `db reset`. Don't reach for `insider-thai-food`; it's seeded only by a separate script (`scripts/seed-insider-packs.ts`) that's not part of the migration chain.
  - apps/insider scaffolding mirrors apps/headball but skips jsdom because no React tests run here yet. Phase 5b will likely add an apps/insider/playwright.config.ts and a separate `vitest.setup.ts` if React component tests land in this app.

- **Learnings:**
  - Insider role tables follow a simpler shape than `game_insider_round`: roles are PUBLIC so no column-level GRANT/REVOKE dance is needed. Standard `enable RLS + anon SELECT policy + alter publication ... add table <name>` (no column list) is correct for any future Insider table that doesn't carry asymmetric secrets (responses, votes per US-5a.3 will follow this same shape).

This matches the existing `round_state.player_id` convention from migration 0001 — no FK enforcement, just a logical reference. The PK `(room_id, round_number, player_id)` plus the rooms-FK-cascade is enough to keep the table consistent.
  - PK violation surfaces as Postgres SQLSTATE `23505` (unique_violation), separate from check-constraint `23514`. Useful for future tests that want to assert "same player can't have two roles in same round" — the PK enforces it, no extra constraint needed.
  - Test cleanup pattern (delete-by-room-code in beforeAll + afterAll) reused verbatim from `migration-0017-game-insider-round.test.ts`. Continue this for US-040 (responses/votes) and beyond — keeps Insider integration tests hermetic without touching other rooms.
