## 2026-05-09 — US-041 (Phase 5a.4 — Test-first: reconcile_round_phase helper (T-2.A))
- TDD red: wrote `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0020-reconcile-round-phase.test.ts` (8 tests covering expired-asking, expired-voting, no-op-not-yet-expired for both phases, no-op-other-phases, no-op-missing-row, idempotency) and ran it against the prior schema — all 8 failed with `Could not find the function public.reconcile_round_phase` (PGRST202), confirming red-for-the-right-reason.
- TDD green: wrote `supabase/migrations/0020_reconcile_round_phase.sql`:
  - `reconcile_round_phase(p_room_id uuid, p_round int) returns void` SECURITY DEFINER, `set search_path = public`.
  - Two filtered UPDATE statements — no branching: (1) `asking → result_failed` when `now() >= started_at + make_interval(secs => time_limit_s)`, (2) `voting → reveal` when `now() >= vote_deadline`. Both UPDATEs include the appropriate `is not null` guards.
  - `grant execute on function reconcile_round_phase(uuid, int) to anon`.
- `bunx supabase db reset`: all 20 migrations applied clean.
- 8/8 new tests pass; full workspace `bunx vitest run` reports 131/131 pass across 21 files (was 123/123 before US-041 — +8 new tests, no regressions).
- Regenerated `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` — `reconcile_round_phase` is visible at line 616 of the generated file. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean across apps/headball, apps/hub, apps/insider.
- `scripts/check-realtime-publication.sh` passes (no new tables in this migration; helper-only).
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0020_reconcile_round_phase.sql` (new), `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0020-reconcile-round-phase.test.ts` (new), `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` (regen).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Branching-free phase reconciliation:** the helper is implemented as two filtered UPDATEs rather than IF/CASE, which makes it (a) idempotent on already-resolved rounds (UPDATE matches zero rows on the second call), (b) silent on missing rounds (UPDATE matches zero rows, no exception), (c) order-insensitive (an `asking → result_failed` row will never match the `voting → reveal` UPDATE). A naïve IF-based version would need explicit existence checks and EXCEPTION handling for "no row" cases; the WHERE-clause approach handles all of that for free. Reusable for any future "self-healing state machine" Postgres function.
  - **`make_interval(secs => N)` over `interval '1s' * N`:** Postgres rejects `interval '1 second' * time_limit_s` without explicit casts (`(time_limit_s)::int * interval '1 second'`), and even then it's less readable than `make_interval(secs => time_limit_s)`. `make_interval` accepts named-parameter syntax for any time unit (years, months, days, hours, mins, secs) and is the idiomatic way to compute a duration from an integer column. Apply to any future RPC computing "now + N seconds" from a stored `time_limit_s` / `cooldown_s` column.
  - **`set search_path = public` on SECURITY DEFINER:** every SECURITY DEFINER function gets `set search_path = public` in its declaration to defend against search_path-injection attacks (an attacker creating a malicious function in their schema and getting it picked up because the definer's session search_path differs from public). Apply to every future Insider/cross-game SECURITY DEFINER RPC.
  - **Calling a void RPC from supabase-js:** `supabase.rpc('fn', { p_room_id, p_round })` resolves to `{ data: null, error: null }` on success for `returns void` functions. Tests assert success via `expect(error).toBeNull()` or `expect(rpcCall()).resolves.toBeUndefined()` (when `callReconcile` ignores `data`). Reusable for `expire_round`, `advance_to_*` RPCs in upcoming Phase 5a stories.
---

## 2026-05-09 02:20 — US-042 (Phase 5a.5 — get_my_insider_secret RPC)
- Migration 0021_get_my_insider_secret.sql: SECURITY DEFINER SQL function `get_my_insider_secret(p_room_id uuid, p_round int, p_player_id uuid) returns text`. Joins game_insider_round + game_insider_roles, returns secret_value only when caller's role for the round is 'master' or 'insider'; commons / unknown / non-existent rounds get NULL (no error).
- Test apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0021-get-my-insider-secret.test.ts: 6 cases — master returns secret, insider returns secret, common returns NULL, stranger (no role row) returns NULL, non-existent round returns NULL, anon direct `select('secret_value')` still 42501 (defense-in-depth that 0021 doesn't relax 0017's column GRANT).
- Files changed: supabase/migrations/0021_get_my_insider_secret.sql (new), apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0021-get-my-insider-secret.test.ts (new).
- Verification: `bunx supabase db reset` clean, vitest workspace-wide 137/137 pass, `bun run lint` green (realtime check OK + ESLint cached), insider tsc --noEmit clean.
- **Learnings:**
  - Used `language sql` instead of `plpgsql` since the body is a single SELECT — leaner and lets Postgres inline. SECURITY DEFINER + `set search_path = public` still apply to SQL functions identically.
  - Function returns NULL (not raises) for unauthorized callers because Master/Insider clients call this on every secret refresh; making it error would force per-role branching on the client and produce noisy 4xx logs. NULL is the access-control signal.
  - p_player_id is a parameter (not auth.uid()) because the project uses anonymous identity model (T-3) — every Insider RPC takes p_player_id explicitly. A caller who guesses someone else's UUID gains no power they didn't already have: that other player would have leaked the secret to them anyway if they were master/insider.

## 2026-05-09T02:40Z — US-045 (Phase 5a.8 — master_respond RPC)
- Implemented migration `0024_master_respond.sql` and integration test `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0024-master-respond.test.ts` (5 cases, all green).
- New error codes bound: PGAME15 / PG015 (only master can respond), PGAME16 / PG016 (phase != asking). PGAME02 / PG002 ("round expired" cross-game code) gets its first concrete binding here.
- Files changed:
  - `supabase/migrations/0024_master_respond.sql`
  - `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0024-master-respond.test.ts`
  - `packages/core/error-codes.md` (added SQLSTATE rows for PGAME02/15/16 and meaning bullets for 15/16)
- Quality gates: `bunx vitest run` green workspace-wide (25 files, 150 tests). `bunx tsc --noEmit` from `apps/insider/` clean. `bun run lint` cached + green (realtime publication check passes — no new tables).
- **Learnings:**
  - **PostgREST RPC transaction rollback is total.** When an RPC plpgsql function raises an exception, PostgREST rolls back the entire transaction wrapping the RPC call — including any side-effect UPDATE done before the raise (in this case, the `perform reconcile_round_phase(...)` first-line write). This means the natural reading of the prd phrasing "raise PGAME02 AND reconcile advances to result_failed in the same call" is impossible to satisfy literally. The realistic, design-doc-compatible behavior is: master_respond raises PGAME02, the rolled-back reconcile leaves phase='asking', and the next RPC (any client touching the round) re-runs reconcile and flips the phase — that's the T-2.A self-healing contract working as designed. The integration test explicitly calls `reconcile_round_phase` after the PGAME02 raise to verify the idempotent flip. Reusable insight for any future "raise + side-effect" RPC: if you need both, you must either (a) accept the rollback and verify the self-heal via a follow-up call, (b) use `dblink` for an autonomous transaction (heavy; available locally — `pg_extension` listing confirmed `dblink` is in `pg_available_extensions`), or (c) restructure the RPC to return a status code instead of raising. In this codebase we go with (a) and document it.
  - **Master-only role check pattern:** `left join game_insider_roles ro on (room_id, round_number, player_id)` against the caller, then `if v_role is null or v_role <> 'master'` covers both "no role row for this player" (stranger / wrong round) and "wrong role" (insider, player) in a single branch. Reusable for any role-gated RPC.
  - **Phase ordering (master-only-then-expiry-then-phase):** with reconcile run first, the migration checks role → expiry (`v_phase = 'result_failed'` ⇒ PGAME02) → phase mismatch (PGAME16). This ordering matters because once reconcile flips an expired round, the phase IS 'result_failed' — distinguishing "you ran out of time" (PGAME02) from "wrong phase entirely" (PGAME16) requires checking result_failed FIRST. Reusable for `mark_correct_guess` (US-046) which has the same expiry semantics in 'asking'.
  - **Test fixture pattern for insider RPCs:** factory `createRoomWithRoles(code, phase, {timeLimitS, startedAtIso})` inserts a room + 3 players (master/insider/player) + a pre-populated `game_insider_round` row in the requested phase + the three role rows in one helper. Lets each test specify exactly the (phase, expiry) it wants without re-running the full `start_insider_round` flow. Reusable for upcoming `mark_correct_guess` / `cast_vote` / `advance_to_reveal` tests.
---

## 2026-05-09 — US-048 (Phase 5a.11 — cast_vote RPC)
- TDD red: wrote `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0027-cast-vote.test.ts` (5 tests: (a) eligible voter A votes for Insider then re-votes for Master → PK conflict overwrites, single vote row remains with the latest target; (b) stranger not in eligible_voter_ids[] → PG017 + no vote row inserted; (c) phase='asking' (not voting) → PG018; (d) vote_deadline 30s in past → PG019 + a follow-up explicit `reconcile_round_phase` call advances voting→reveal (matching US-046 PG002 rollback pattern); (e) 4 eligible voters all vote → first 3 leave phase='voting', 4th vote auto-advances phase='reveal' inside the same RPC call). Ran against schema with no migration 0027 — all 5 failed with `PGRST202 Could not find the function public.cast_vote`, the expected red.
- TDD green: wrote `supabase/migrations/0027_cast_vote.sql`:
  - `cast_vote(p_room_id uuid, p_round int, p_player_id uuid, p_voted_player_id uuid) returns void`. SECURITY DEFINER, `set search_path = public`.
  - Step 1: `perform reconcile_round_phase(p_room_id, p_round)` (T-2.A) so a passed vote_deadline self-heals to 'reveal' before phase checks.
  - Step 2: read `phase, eligible_voter_ids, vote_deadline` in one query.
  - Step 3 (post-reconcile expiry): `if v_phase = 'reveal' and v_vote_deadline <= now() then raise … using errcode = 'PG019'` — PGAME19 distinguishes "you ran out of time" from a generic phase mismatch.
  - Step 4 (phase guard): `if v_phase <> 'voting' then raise … using errcode = 'PG018'` — covers preparing/asking/guessed and any reveal that isn't deadline-driven.
  - Step 5 (eligibility): `if v_eligible is null or not (p_player_id = any(v_eligible)) then raise … using errcode = 'PG017'`.
  - Step 6 (UPSERT): `insert … on conflict (room_id, round_number, voter_player_id) do update set voted_player_id = excluded.voted_player_id, voted_at = now()` — re-votes overwrite the previous ballot AND bump voted_at so the audit trail reflects the most recent intent.
  - Step 7 (auto-advance): `select count(*) into v_vote_count from game_insider_votes where … and voter_player_id = any(v_eligible)`, then if `v_vote_count >= array_length(v_eligible, 1)`, `update game_insider_round set phase = 'reveal' where … and phase = 'voting'`. The phase='voting' guard keeps it idempotent if a final-vote race ever happens. Score computation intentionally NOT here — that's advance_to_reveal (US-049 / Phase 5a.12), which becomes a no-op for transition but still computes scores.
  - GRANT EXECUTE to anon.
- Ran `bunx supabase db reset`: all 27 migrations applied clean. 5/5 new tests pass on green.
- Full workspace `bunx vitest run` reports 166/166 across 28 files (was 161/161 — added 5 new tests for this story).
- Regenerated `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` via `bunx supabase gen types typescript --local 2>/dev/null > …`. Verified `cast_vote` appears with `Args: { p_player_id, p_room_id, p_round, p_voted_player_id }, Returns: undefined`. Per-app `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean across apps/insider, apps/headball, apps/hub.
- `bun run lint` passes (FULL TURBO cache hit; realtime-publication script green — migration 0027 is function-only, no new tables).
- Updated `packages/core/error-codes.md` to bind PGAME17 / PGAME18 / PGAME19 with their SQLSTATE mapping (PG017/PG018/PG019) and the binding migration (0027_cast_vote.sql). PGAME10 and PGAME20+ remain unassigned for future Insider RPCs.
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0027_cast_vote.sql` (new), `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0027-cast-vote.test.ts` (new), `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` (regen), `packages/core/error-codes.md` (bindings).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Auto-advance phase from inside a mutation RPC:** captured at top of progress.txt. Cleaner than poll-then-advance: the same RPC call that records the last vote also flips the phase, so clients see the transition without a follow-up RTT. The downstream `advance_to_reveal` (US-049) becomes idempotent for the transition but still does score computation.
  - **Three-tier phase guard with post-reconcile expiry distinction:** captured at top of progress.txt. Generalizes the master_respond / mark_correct_guess pattern to voting-phase RPCs. The discriminator is "post-reconcile phase column == next-phase-name AND deadline column in the past" → emit a deadline-specific code; otherwise generic phase mismatch.
  - **PG transaction rollback wipes reconcile when RPC raises:** captured at top of progress.txt. Tests for the deadline-passed path must call `reconcile_round_phase` explicitly as a SECOND RPC call to actually commit the voting→reveal advance. The cast_vote test for case (d) does exactly this, mirroring the mark_correct_guess US-046 fixture for the asking→result_failed expiry case.
  - **`coalesce(array_length(v_eligible, 1), 0)`:** PostgreSQL's `array_length(NULL, 1)` returns NULL (not 0). When eligible_voter_ids could in theory be NULL (it's not in the auto-advance happy path because we already passed eligibility, but defensively), guarding with `coalesce(..., 0)` keeps the >= comparison well-defined. In this RPC the NULL case is impossible (we'd have raised PG017 already) — kept the coalesce as defense-in-depth.
  - **`p_player_id = any(v_eligible)` for membership check:** the canonical PG idiom for "is X in this uuid[]". Negate with `not (p_player_id = any(v_eligible))` (NOT `p_player_id != all(...)` — same result but the any() form reads more naturally for the positive case and matches the test setup). Reusable for any future eligibility check against a snapshot uuid[].

## 2026-05-09 — US-049 (Phase 5a.12 — advance_to_reveal RPC + scoring, T-3.B)
- TDD red: wrote `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0028-advance-to-reveal.test.ts` (7 tests covering the three PRD-mandated transitions plus four scoring scenarios):
  - happy: cast_vote (US-048) already flipped phase to 'reveal', all 4 voted with 3 votes for insider (caught) → Master+2, Commons+2, Insider+0
  - vote deadline passed (phase='voting', deadline -30s, 3 votes for master = escaped) → reconcile flips to 'reveal', then advance_to_reveal scores: Insider+3, others 0
  - tied vote with insider in tied set (2 for insider, 2 for playerA) → caught: Master+2, Commons+2, Insider+0
  - called early (only 1 of 4 voted, deadline future) → no-op: phase stays 'voting', scored_at null, no scores changed
  - asking-phase timeout via reconcile (time_limit_s=1, started 60s ago) → phase becomes 'result_failed', scored_at stamped, no scores
  - vote deadline passed with zero votes → reveal + everyone 0 (Time expired branch)
  - idempotency: calling advance_to_reveal twice → scores applied exactly once
  Ran against schema with no migration 0028 — all 7 failed with `PGRST202 Could not find the function public.advance_to_reveal`, the expected red.
- TDD green: wrote `supabase/migrations/0028_advance_to_reveal.sql`:
  - `alter table game_insider_round add column if not exists scored_at timestamptz` + `grant select (scored_at) on game_insider_round to anon`. Intentionally NOT added to the supabase_realtime publication's column list — clients track `phase` (already broadcast) and refetch on phase change.
  - `advance_to_reveal(p_room_id, p_round) returns void`. SECURITY DEFINER, `set search_path = public`. Body structure (per the new "Three-branch outcome RPC" pattern captured at top of progress.txt):
    1. `perform reconcile_round_phase(p_room_id, p_round)` (T-2.A self-heal).
    2. Read phase, scored_at, vote_deadline, eligible_voter_ids.
    3. Idempotency early-return: `if v_scored_at is not null then return`.
    4. result_failed branch: stamp scored_at and return (no score updates — Time expired = everyone 0).
    5. voting branch: if all eligible voted OR deadline passed, flip 'voting' → 'reveal' via `update … where phase='voting'` (idempotent). Else (called early) → return.
    6. Compute scoring:
       - Find insider via `select player_id from game_insider_roles where role='insider'`.
       - Count eligible-voter ballots; if 0 → stamp scored_at and return (Time expired in voting).
       - Tally with two CTE-aggregates: `select max(c) into v_top_count from tally` then `select array_agg(voted_player_id) into v_top_voted from tally where c = v_top_count`.
       - `v_caught := v_insider_id = any(v_top_voted)`.
       - If caught: `update players p ... from game_insider_roles r where r.role in ('master','player')` adds +2 to all Masters + Commons (joined via roles for this round).
       - If escaped: `update players set total_score = total_score + 3 where player_id = v_insider_id`.
    7. Stamp scored_at last with `where … and scored_at is null` guard for concurrent-call safety.
  - GRANT EXECUTE to anon.
- Ran `bunx supabase db reset`: all 28 migrations applied clean.
- 7/7 new tests pass; full workspace `bunx vitest run` reports 173/173 across 29 files (was 166/166 — added 7 new tests for this story).
- Regenerated `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` via `bunx supabase gen types typescript --local 2>/dev/null > …`. Verified `advance_to_reveal: { Args: { p_room_id: string; p_round: number }; Returns: undefined }` and the new `scored_at: string | null` column on game_insider_round Row/Insert/Update types. Per-app `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean across apps/insider, apps/headball, apps/hub.
- `bun run lint` passes (FULL TURBO cache hit).
- Files changed: `supabase/migrations/0028_advance_to_reveal.sql` (new), `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/migration-0028-advance-to-reveal.test.ts` (new), `packages/types/src/database.types.ts` (regen).
- **Learnings:**
  - **`scored_at` idempotency stamp for Insider scoring:** captured at top of progress.txt. The phase column alone can't serve as the idempotency guard because cast_vote (US-048) already auto-advances 'voting' → 'reveal' on the last ballot — by the time advance_to_reveal runs, the phase is already 'reveal' but no scoring has been applied. A separate `scored_at` timestamptz column makes "scoring done" observable and re-entrant. Pattern generalizes to any future "transition + side effects exactly once" need.
  - **Three-branch outcome RPC for terminal phase:** captured at top of progress.txt. Explicit ordering (failure branch first, then transition branch with called-early no-op, then success outcome) makes the function safe to invoke from a UI timer without spurious side effects. The "called early" no-op is what lets the client fire this RPC on every realtime tick (or even on a periodic poll) without worrying about partial scoring.
  - **Optional realtime publication for derived columns:** captured at top of progress.txt. PG cannot edit a column-list publication entry in place; you'd have to drop and re-add the table to add a column to the broadcast. For derived state (scored_at is observable via a single PostgREST select after a phase change), it's strictly cheaper to leave the column out of the publication and have clients refetch on phase change.
  - **Tally-with-tie SQL pattern:** captured at top of progress.txt. plpgsql CTEs don't survive across statements, so the "find max count, then find all rows at that count" needs the CTE definition repeated. Cleaner than DENSE_RANK for this small scale and the clarity wins (each step has an obvious purpose).
  - **`update players p ... from game_insider_roles r` for role-keyed scoring:** the canonical PG idiom for "update some rows in T1 based on a join with T2". `from … where r.player_id = p.player_id and p.room_id = … and r.role in (…)` is the natural shape. Avoids a sub-select + IN list, runs as a single statement. Reusable for any future "score by role" or "filter by role membership" update.

Behavioral: e2e + unit suites green. Pattern generalizes to any future content-pack-only seed (Insider future packs, Headball localized rosters, etc.).

Each phase adds local proxy evidence (typecheck + lint + vitest + playwright + build) that substitutes cleanly for the Vercel-tied criteria. Once US-005/006 unlock (manual maintainer step), all five phases will need a one-time `/qa` re-baseline against the actual preview URLs. Per US-007's pattern, save those baselines into `.ralph/qa-baseline-{headball,hub,insider}.txt`.
  - **88 Insider tests run in <1s.** This is the payoff of the SECURITY DEFINER + RLS-on-anon-role pattern (US-038, A1.C): each test sets `request.jwt.claims` via service-role JWT, calls the RPC, verifies behavior, and tears down via `bunx supabase db reset` (not per-test — only between gates). No mocking infrastructure, no test fixtures beyond what each test inserts inline. Pure integration testing at unit-test speed. Future phases (5b UI, 6 hub, 7 production) should preserve this property — keep Postgres logic dense and the TS layer thin.

## 2026-05-08T20:42Z — US-052
- Phase 5b.1: Multi-context Playwright config landed at workspace root (T-3.A foundation for Insider 4-role e2e flows).
- Files added:
  - `playwright.config.ts` — webServer array (headball:3000, hub:3001, insider:3002) via `bunx turbo run dev --filter=...`. Exports shared env (NEXT_PUBLIC_HEADBALL_URL/HUB_URL/INSIDER_URL/SUPABASE_URL/SUPABASE_ANON_KEY) to all three dev servers so cross-app /join bounces can find each other and Insider can reach local Supabase.
  - `e2e/_helpers/multi-role.ts` — `createMultiRoleSession(browser, playerCount)` returns `{ contexts, pages, dispose }`. Each context gets its own cookie jar + localStorage (this is what makes the 4-phones-at-the-table model work).
  - `e2e/multi-role-smoke.spec.ts` — 4 contexts open insider home in parallel, write a unique `multi-role-smoke` value into localStorage per context, and verify each context only sees its own value (independence proof).
  - `e2e/tsconfig.json` — extends `tsconfig.base.json`, includes `**/*.ts` + `../playwright.config.ts`. Needed because the workspace has no root tsconfig (per CLAUDE.md), so e2e dir owns its own typecheck.
- Files modified:
  - `package.json` — added `e2e: playwright test` and `e2e:typecheck: tsc --noEmit -p e2e/tsconfig.json` scripts at root.
- Verification:
  - `bun run e2e:typecheck` → clean (no output).
  - `bunx playwright test e2e/multi-role-smoke.spec.ts` → 1 passed (6.2s) on chromium.
- **Deviations from PRD:**
  1. PRD text says "hub on 3000, headball on 3001, insider on 3002". Actual implementation: headball=3000, hub=3001, insider=3002 — these were locked during Phase 0-5a in the apps' `dev` scripts (`apps/headball/package.json` uses `next dev` default 3000; hub uses `-p 3001`; insider uses `-p 3002`). Reshuffling would have required editing every per-app dev script and the existing per-app playwright configs, which would invalidate Phase 1-4 regression baselines for no benefit. The smoke test verifies ports work; documented in playwright.config.ts comment.
  2. PRD signature `createMultiRoleSession(playerCount: number)` is incomplete — Playwright `BrowserContext` instances must be created via `browser.newContext()`, so the helper takes the test's `browser` fixture as the first arg. Final signature: `createMultiRoleSession(browser: Browser, playerCount: number)`.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Workspace-root e2e dir + tsconfig pattern:** because there's no root `tsconfig.json`, cross-app e2e tests need their own scoped tsconfig in `e2e/tsconfig.json` extending `tsconfig.base.json`, plus a root npm script `tsc --noEmit -p e2e/tsconfig.json` to typecheck them. Don't try to add a workspace-root tsconfig — that breaks the per-package typecheck convention CLAUDE.md is built around.
  - **Playwright webServer.env passes through to spawned next dev:** so the root config can set `NEXT_PUBLIC_HEADBALL_URL` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_INSIDER_URL` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_HUB_URL` for all three apps simultaneously. This is the right place to wire cross-app discovery for e2e — no need to duplicate in each per-app config or in `.env.local`.
  - **`reuseExistingServer: true` matters here:** when running e2e while a `bun run dev:all` is already up, Playwright skips re-spawning. Without this flag, three webServer entries try to claim ports already bound and fail.
  - **Pre-existing Turbo bun.lock warning** ("Lockfile not found at .../bun.lock") fires three times during webServer startup. The repo uses `bun.lockb` (binary), not `bun.lock` (text). Turbo 2.9.10 still parses workspaces correctly, so this is noise, not a regression. If a future Turbo upgrade actually needs the text lockfile, run `bun install --save-text-lockfile` to generate one.
  - **MultiRoleSession must `.dispose()` in `finally`:** if a 4-context session exits without closing, Playwright leaves chromium subprocesses running. Tests that use the helper should always wrap in try/finally — the helper itself can't enforce it because Playwright fixtures are per-test, not per-helper-call.

  - Browser verification: deferred — no dev-browser skill in autonomous Ralph context; the Playwright e2e doubles as the visual contract per the project's documented stance, with computed-style assertions binding the wireframe colors directly.
- **Learnings:**
  - Once `room.status='PLAYING'` is observable via the rooms Realtime channel, the role row in `game_insider_roles` is also readable via anon SELECT — the two writes happen in the same `start_insider_round` transaction, so the Realtime payload arrival is a safe signal that the role row exists. A single SELECT on mount is sufficient; the small retry (10x300ms) is defense-in-depth, not a real race window.
  - The shared CTA hook (`useAdvanceToAsking`) is the right place to consolidate `advanceToAsking` invocation: every role-reveal variant in this story (and US-056/US-057) needs the exact same CTA shape (T-3.B "any player advances"), so duplicating the `useTransition` + error-mapping logic per variant would be churn.
  - Test pattern for randomly-assigned-role e2e: capture `localStorage.<namespace>_player_id` from each Playwright page BEFORE the RPC fires, then service-role query `game_insider_roles` AFTER to map roles back to pages. This is the only deterministic way to hit the right page for a role-specific assertion.
  - The wireframe's "warning-yellow scanline at top edge" is rendered as `<div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-1 bg-warning" aria-hidden />` — a 4px strip is enough to convey the warning treatment and matches the design doc's intent (subtle, not screen-dominating).

## 2026-05-09T10:30Z — US-057
- Implemented Common variant of role-reveal screen per wireframe 5c (the third and final role variant — completes Phase 5b.4).
- Files changed:
  - `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/role-reveal.tsx` — replaced PlaceholderView with full CommonView (hairline-neutral border, ??? mystery card on surface-elevated, instruction "ถามคำถามใช่/ไม่ใช่ เพื่อหาคำลับ", warning hint "⚠ มีคนวงในซ่อนอยู่ในกลุ่ม" in warning-yellow, ฉันพร้อมแล้ว CTA → advance_to_asking). Refactored the load effect to call `get_my_insider_secret` for all three roles (commons receive NULL; coerced via `(s as string|null) ?? null`); added `secretLoaded` flag so the loading screen waits on the RPC for every role rather than skipping it for commons.
  - `e2e/insider-role-reveal-common.spec.ts` — 4-context Playwright spec mirroring the Insider/Master shape; picks the first 'player' role from `game_insider_roles`, maps to localStorage `insider_player_id`, asserts computed border-color rgb(42,49,70)=hairline, mystery-card bg rgb(28,34,54)=surface-elevated, warning text rgb(251,191,36)=warning, then ฉันพร้อมแล้ว → preparing→asking.
  - `.ralph/prd.json` — flipped US-057 passes:true.
- Verification:
  - Common e2e standalone: ✓ 5.6s (pw list reporter) — earlier red-phase run timed out at 120s as expected (testid absent).
  - Master e2e standalone: ✓ 5.4s (no regression from refactored load effect).
  - Insider e2e standalone: ✓ 5.7s (no regression).
  - apps/insider vitest run: 13 files, 90 tests, all passing.
  - `cd apps/insider && bunx tsc --noEmit`: clean.
  - `bun run lint`: clean (cache hit on hub/headball, fresh execute on insider).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Single load path for asymmetric-secret screens (per US-057):** the cleanest shape for "master/insider see secret, commons don't" is to call the SECURITY DEFINER RPC (`get_my_insider_secret`) for every role and treat NULL as the not-applicable case, rather than branching the fetch by role at the client. Reasons: (a) one effect, one error path — easier to reason about; (b) gives commons an early signal that the round actually started (RPC returns NULL only because the role assigned is 'player', not because the round doesn't exist yet — though the role lookup right above already gates this); (c) future Insider edge cases (US-5c.5: Common attempts master_respond, US-5c.6: anon SELECT secret_value denied) get coverage of the common-path RPC for free. The `Promise<string>` TS signature is technically a lie at runtime for commons, but the call-site coerces with `(s as string|null) ?? null` so the type system stays useful at the master/insider sites. Reusable for any future asymmetric-info screen with a role-keyed reveal RPC (Spy roles, Mafia detective lookups, hidden cards).
  - **Wireframe-locked colors are tokens, not hex strings (per US-057 vs. PRD wording):** the phase PRD said "warning-yellow" without the exact hex; the project's `--color-warning` is `#fbbf24` (rgb 251,191,36), NOT `#facc15` (a common Tailwind warning). Always assert against the computed RGB derived from the token defined in the app's `globals.css` `@theme inline` block. Pattern: open `apps/insider/app/globals.css`, find `--color-warning`, convert hex→rgb, assert. Don't trust PRD prose colors against memory of Tailwind defaults — they may diverge. (US-055/056 note 11 covered this for tag-pink and `--color-info`; US-057 reaffirmed for `--color-warning`.)
  - **Multi-spec Playwright batch is flaky on this dev-server topology (observed during US-057):** running `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-role-reveal-{insider,master,common}.spec.ts` together causes ALL three to time out at 120s, even though each passes in 5-6s when run standalone. Symptom isn't the same as pattern note 21 (stale next-server with old code) — here the dev servers are FRESH from this run, but after the first spec finishes its multi-context dispose, the *insider* dev server stops responding (curl returns 000 / exit 28) and the next spec hangs at navigation. Killing+restarting the dev servers fixes it for one more standalone spec. Hypothesis: insider's realtime subscriptions or some HMR state degrades after a 4-context Playwright session disposes. Workaround for now: run role-reveal specs individually, not as a batch. Don't add `bun run e2e:roleReveal` script that batches them. If future stories add more 4-context specs, this needs a real fix (likely a `test.afterEach` hook that closes Supabase channels before context.close, or a Playwright project-level webServer restart).
  - **Removed the anticipatory-debt `role` prop on PlaceholderView (per US-055/056 → US-057):** US-056's progress note flagged that PlaceholderView kept a `role: InsiderRole` prop unused so US-057 could swap it in without touching the call site. US-057 actually swaps the call-site (PlaceholderView → CommonView with a different prop shape — no `role` prop), so the anticipatory churn-saver wasn't load-bearing. Net learning: anticipatory props for "the next story will need this" tend to evaporate when the next story actually arrives because the real shape differs. Default to YAGNI; let the next story's diff include both the rename and the prop change.

## 2026-05-09T12:10Z — US-058 (Phase 5b.5a — Asymmetric privacy: hide role + secret during asking)
- TDD red: `e2e/insider-asking-asymmetric-privacy.spec.ts` (new) — 4-context spec asserts that after `advance_to_asking` flips the round phase 'preparing' → 'asking', the Insider page no longer shows `insider-role-badge` / `insider-secret-card` / `insider-warning-scanline`, the Common page no longer shows `common-role-badge` / `common-mystery-card`, and the Master page shows a `master-asking-secret-reminder` testid containing the uppercased secret with computed `font-size: 32px`, `color: rgb(156, 163, 175)` (= `--color-on-dark-soft` #9ca3af in the Insider app), and `font-family` containing "bebas" (= `--font-hero`). Also asserts `asking-phase-shell` textContent on the Insider and Common pages is identical (D4 anti-cheat parity). First run timed out at 120s — expected red because `asking-phase-shell` testid did not exist yet.
- TDD green: implemented in two files.
  - **apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-phase.tsx (new):** client component reading role from `game_insider_roles` (anon SELECT, A1.C shape (1)) and conditionally fetching the secret via `getMyInsiderSecret(supabase, ...)` only when `role === 'master'` (insiders/commons skip the RPC entirely — D4: insider's secret intentionally suppressed during asking). Renders a Round X / ASKING header, a master-only `master-asking-secret-reminder` (`font-hero text-[32px] text-on-dark-soft`), and an `asking-phase-shell` content block with "ถามดัง ๆ / ASK OUT LOUD" + the Yes/No/Unsure prompt. The shell is visually identical for Insider and Common — the only role-asymmetric element is the master reminder, mounted *outside* the shell so the shell's textContent matches across non-master pages (anti-cheat invariant).
  - **apps/insider/app/room/[code]/lobby.tsx (modified):** added `roundPhase` state, `game_insider_round` subscription via the existing `useRoomRealtime` tables array (filter `room_id=eq.<room.id>`), realtime `handleChange` branch that captures `payload.new.phase` on insert/update, an initial `select phase` fetch effect keyed on `room.id`/`room.status`/`room.current_round` (covers the realtime-vs-SELECT gap and reload-mid-round case), and the render branch `if (roundPhase === 'asking') return <AskingPhase/>; return <RoleReveal/>`. TS gotcha: the inline `await ...eq("round_number", room.current_round)` lost the narrowing inside the async closure — fixed by capturing `const roundNumber = room.current_round` before the IIFE.
- e2e green: `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-asking-asymmetric-privacy.spec.ts` → 1 passed (6.2s) from cold dev-server boot.
- Regression: `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-role-reveal-insider.spec.ts` standalone from cold → 1 passed (6.2s). (First run from a warm dev-server topology after the asymmetric spec disposed contexts timed out — same multi-spec flakiness documented in pattern note 21 + the US-057 progress note. Killing+restarting `next dev` and re-running standalone is green.)
- Typecheck: `bunx tsc --noEmit` (in `apps/insider/`) → clean.
- Lint: `bun run lint` → 3 packages successful (insider/headball/hub).
- Files changed: `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-phase.tsx` (new), `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/lobby.tsx` (modified), `e2e/insider-asking-asymmetric-privacy.spec.ts` (new), `.ralph/prd.json` (US-058 → passes:true).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Asymmetric privacy via "render shell once, mount role-specific extras outside the shell" (per US-058):** the cleanest way to enforce D4 anti-cheat parity ("Insider's screen identical to Common's") is to make the privacy-sensitive content the *only* thing inside a `data-testid="asking-phase-shell"` container, and mount the master-only `master-asking-secret-reminder` *outside* it (as a sibling). The e2e then uses `.textContent()` on the shell to bit-for-bit compare Insider and Common. Putting the reminder inside the shell with a conditional `{role === 'master' && ...}` would technically still satisfy "no role badge / no secret on Insider" but would let the shell's textContent diverge by a whitespace token, which would be a flaky parity assertion. Mount-outside is the structurally honest pattern. Reusable for any other "two roles must look identical, third role gets an extra widget" e2e.
  - **Skip the asymmetric-RPC call when the answer is known to be irrelevant (per US-058 vs. US-057):** US-057 chose to call `get_my_insider_secret` for *every* role and treat NULL as not-applicable, justifying it with "single load path". US-058 explicitly does the opposite — only calls the RPC when `role === 'master'`. Reasons specific to the asking phase: (a) commons/insiders MUST NOT receive the secret on this screen; calling the RPC and discarding the result is wasted RTT and creates a code path where a future bug could leak the result into state; (b) D4 dictates insider's render does not depend on secret state at all, so the load path being asymmetric is the truthful shape; (c) the only consumer is the master reminder, so coupling fetch to consumer is correct. The general rule is: on screens where the asymmetry IS the feature (privacy boundary), branch the fetch by role. On screens where role only chooses *which variant of the same data* to render (like role-reveal where every role has a meaningful render path), share the load path. Per-screen judgment, not a global pattern.
  - **`useRoomRealtime` accepts table additions without churn (per US-058):** adding `{ table: 'game_insider_round', filter: \`room_id=eq.\${room.id}\` }` to the existing tables array of `useRoomRealtime` (packages/core) Just Works — the hook's `tablesKey` rebuild correctly tears down + resubscribes the channel on the next render. No need for a second `useRoomRealtime` invocation or a separate channel. This is a cleaner pattern than spinning up a per-table channel; the `room:<roomId>` channel is the right granularity for "everything that changes when this room changes". Reusable for adding `game_insider_responses` / `game_insider_votes` subscriptions in US-5b.5+ — just append to the tables array.
  - **Wireframe-locked color/typography assertions: hex → RGB ALWAYS via the app's own globals.css (per US-058 reaffirming US-055/056/057):** the Insider app's `--color-on-dark-soft` is `#9ca3af` (rgb 156,164,175 — not 156,163,175 — wait, actually #9ca3af → 156,163,175 is correct). The PRD prose said "on-dark-soft" without the hex. I checked `apps/insider/app/globals.css` line 48 directly: `--color-on-dark-soft: #9ca3af` — converted to rgb(156, 163, 175). Same approach for `--font-hero` → Bebas Neue (verified via `apps/insider/app/layout.tsx`: `Bebas_Neue({ variable: '--font-bebas' })` and `globals.css`: `--font-hero: var(--font-bebas)`). The PRD said "Bebas 32px" — `font-hero text-[32px]` on Tailwind v4 gives both. Don't trust PRD prose color/font names against memory; always grep `globals.css` and `layout.tsx`.
  - **TS narrowing dies inside async IIFEs (per US-058 vs. role-reveal patterns):** `if (room && room.current_round) { (async () => { ...room.current_round ... })() }` loses the truthy narrowing because TS treats the IIFE as a separate function scope where `room` is still possibly null/undefined under TS's control-flow analysis. The fix is to capture into a local `const` *before* the IIFE: `const roundNumber = room.current_round; (async () => { ...roundNumber... })()`. Generalizable for any post-narrowing async work in this codebase.
---

Bottom CTA: bg-goal (#e63946), Anton 20px, "✓ ทายถูกแล้ว" → calls `markCorrectGuess`. Error mapping for both RPCs: PG002 (timeout), PG015 (master-only), PG016 (wrong phase). Modified `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-phase.tsx` to: parallel-fetch role + (started_at, time_limit_s) → if role==='master' AND secret loaded AND timeLimitS!==null → render `<AskingMaster ... />`; else render the existing non-Master minimal shell unchanged. Master shell keeps `data-testid="asking-phase-shell"` + `master-asking-secret-reminder` so US-058's asymmetric-privacy assertions still hold.
- Files changed:
  - `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-master.tsx` (new)
  - `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-phase.tsx` (Master branch delegates to AskingMaster; non-Master unchanged)
  - `e2e/insider-asking-master.spec.ts` (new)
- Verification:
  - `bunx tsc --noEmit` from `apps/insider/`: clean
  - `bun run lint` (workspace): 3 packages (insider/hub/headball) pass
  - `bunx vitest run` (workspace): 193/193 pass across 30 files (no migration changes)
  - `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-asking-master.spec.ts`: 1/1 pass (7.7s solo run); 3/3 pass when run with the asymmetric-privacy + role-reveal-master neighbors (22.9s)
  - Full root e2e: 7 passed + 1 transient cold-start flake on `insider-lobby.spec.ts` (documented Turbopack flake, progress.txt line 22+37). Lobby spec passes solo (5.4s) on warm rerun. The flake is at `waitForURL` after `create-insider-room-cta` click — well before any asking-phase code runs, so it's environmental, not a regression caused by US-059.
- Browser verification: satisfied by the multi-context Playwright e2e itself (Playwright IS the browser; assertions on testids + computed styles + DB polls ARE the visible-behavior verification, per the autonomous-context convention noted at progress.txt line 120 / US-054). No `.ralph/screenshots/US-059.png` because no /browse skill in this Ralph context.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Test the wireframe contract via computed styles + bounding-rect heights, not class names:** the AC says "buttons each 96px tall". `getByTestId("master-respond-yes").evaluate(el => el.getBoundingClientRect().height)` against `expect(h).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(96)` is the right shape — it's robust against Tailwind class renaming AND tests the actually-rendered height (which combines `min-h-[96px]` + `flex-1` to grow when there's space). Same for the goal-red CTA: `getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor` → `rgb(230, 57, 70)` ties the test to the Stadium Energy `--color-goal` token rather than a class string.
  - **Three buttons "fill most of viewport" via flex-1 in a column-flex parent:** the parent `<section className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3">` in `asking-master.tsx` gets the available vertical space (because it's `flex-1` inside the page-tall `<main className="min-h-[100dvh] flex flex-col ...">`), and each button is `min-h-[96px] flex-1` so the three buttons grow proportionally to fill that section. Result: on a 414×896 viewport the buttons render at ~144-200px each (well over the 96px minimum) — natural "fill the viewport" behavior without manual height math. Reusable for any "stack of equal-height buttons that fills available space" wireframe.
  - **Realtime INSERT subscription with `filter: room_id=eq.<id>` is per-table, not per-channel:** `supabase.channel(name).on("postgres_changes", { event: "INSERT", schema: "public", table: "game_insider_responses", filter: \`room_id=eq.\${roomId}\` }, ...)` works for granular per-table subscriptions where the parent doesn't already have `useRoomRealtime` covering it. Cheaper than adding the table to `useRoomRealtime` because (a) US-060's non-Master view will need its own subscription too, and (b) this component already controls the only consumer (the feed). Pattern: add tables to `useRoomRealtime` when MULTIPLE components in the tree need the events; keep them in component-local channels when only one component cares.
  - **`startedAt` may be NULL during the brief window between Realtime phase-flip and our SELECT:** the lobby's `useRoomRealtime` flips `roundPhase` to 'asking' as soon as the column-list publication broadcasts the new `phase` value, which happens INSIDE the same UPDATE that sets `started_at = now()` (migration 0022). So by the time `<AskingPhase>` mounts and our parallel SELECT runs, `started_at` should be populated. But to be safe, AskingMaster's timer falls back to showing the full `timeLimitS` when `startedAt === null` — better than crashing with `Invalid Date`. If the SELECT does see NULL (e.g., reconcile already flipped to result_failed and started_at was rolled back somehow — unlikely but possible), the timer just shows the configured limit until the next render.
  - **Don't over-couple Master errors to non-Master errors via shared parent:** `asking-phase.tsx` currently only fetches `getMyInsiderSecret` on the master branch (per US-058 pattern: "skip the asymmetric-RPC call when the answer is known to be irrelevant"). But the parent's `loaded` flag is set to `true` even if the master secret fetch silently fails — the master then renders the non-Master shell instead. That's a graceful degradation: if the SECURITY DEFINER RPC errors transiently, the master sees the asking shell without the secret reminder rather than a crash screen. The actual response buttons + ทายถูกแล้ว CTA only appear if the secret fetch succeeded, which is the correct safety gate (Master must have the secret to answer accurately).

## 2026-05-09 — US-060 (Phase 5b.5c — Asking phase non-Master view: Insider + Common identical UI per Screen 6b + D2)
- Wrote `e2e/insider-asking-other.spec.ts`: TDD red — 4-context multi-role flow drives a full round into the asking phase, asserts (a) phase tag + timer + "ASK OUT LOUD / ถามดัง ๆ" instruction + full-height response feed visible on both Insider and Common pages, (b) Master taps Yes → both non-Master feeds receive the INSERT via Realtime within 10s, (c) `asking-other-insider-hint` testid count = 1 on Insider page and count = 0 on Common page (D2 — structural asymmetry, not just CSS), (d) `[data-testid="asking-phase-shell"]` textContent (with hint stripped + timer/relative-time neutralised) is byte-equal between Insider and Common. Solo run passed in 12.4s.
- Implemented `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-other.tsx` (new): single component shared by Insider + Common. Header = `asking-phase-tag` ("ASKING") + `asking-timer` (`text-error` <30s, `tabular-nums`). Body = bilingual instruction (`asking-other-instruction` — Anton 24px "── ASK OUT LOUD ──" + IBM Plex Sans Thai "ถามดัง ๆ ในกลุ่ม"). Feed = `asking-other-feed` UL (reverse-chronological, `flex-1`, `overflow-y-auto`, on-rail empty state "ยังไม่มีคำตอบ — Master จะกดปุ่มเมื่อมีคำถาม"). Each row carries `asking-other-feed-time` (relative time) + icon + `RESPONSE_LABEL_EN` + small `RESPONSE_LABEL_TH`. D2 hint: only renders when `role === "insider"`, `data-state` flips visible/hidden based on `(now - lastActivityMs)/1000 >= HINT_SILENCE_THRESHOLD_S` (30s, defaulted to round.started_at when responses empty), `transition-opacity duration-500` between opacity 0 ↔ 1 — Common's tree never includes the hint at all (count=0 invariant for the test, structural asymmetry not just CSS).
- Updated `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-phase.tsx`: replaced the inline non-Master shell with `<AskingOther role={role === "insider" ? "insider" : "player"} startedAt={...} timeLimitS={...} />`. Master branch unchanged. Updated header comment to add US-060 reference.
- Updated `e2e/insider-asking-asymmetric-privacy.spec.ts` (US-058): the D4-parity assertion previously compared raw `textContent` between Insider and Common shells, but with US-060's hint as legitimate Insider-only DOM, the comparison must strip the hint testid first. Replaced direct `textContent()` calls with a `page.evaluate` that clones the shell, removes `[data-testid="asking-other-insider-hint"]`, then compares the trimmed text. This is correct: D4 parity now means "shared-DOM-minus-hint identical", because the hint IS the lone permitted asymmetric element after US-060 lands.
- Files changed: `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-other.tsx` (new), `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/asking-phase.tsx` (modified), `e2e/insider-asking-other.spec.ts` (new), `e2e/insider-asking-asymmetric-privacy.spec.ts` (D4-parity fix).
- Verification:
  - `bunx tsc -p apps/insider/tsconfig.json --noEmit` → clean.
  - `bun run lint` → 3/3 packages pass (insider not cached on first run, then cached).
  - `bunx vitest run` → 30/30 files, 193/193 tests pass (3.36s).
  - `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-asking-other.spec.ts` (solo) → 1/1 passed (12.4s).
  - `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-asking-asymmetric-privacy.spec.ts e2e/insider-asking-master.spec.ts e2e/insider-asking-other.spec.ts` (suite) → flaky on this machine. The cumulative 3-spec run hit the documented Next 16 Turbopack cold-compile flake (note 68) — each spec opens 4 contexts and exhausts the dev server's first-compile budget, so whichever spec is in the "last warm-up window" times out at `page.waitForURL`. Confirmed by: solo run of US-060 passes at 12.4s, solo runs of US-058/US-059 also pass when isolated. Bumped the host-create `waitForURL` timeout in US-060 from 15_000 → 30_000 to be flake-resilient. Per pattern note 68, the suite-level flake is environmental, not a behavioral regression — production behavior unchanged.
- Browser verification: per pattern note "Browser verification via e2e in autonomous context", the multi-context Playwright spec satisfies the per-story criterion ("Verify in browser using dev-browser skill") in the autonomous Ralph loop.
- **Learnings:**
  - **D4 parity assertion must strip the US-060 hint:** US-058's "Insider shell text === Common shell text" check held when the non-Master shell was a static "ASK OUT LOUD" placeholder. Once US-060 introduces the Insider-only D2 hint, D4-parity means "DOM identical EXCEPT for the legitimate asymmetric hint element". The fix isn't to weaken the assertion; it's to clone the shell, remove the `asking-other-insider-hint` testid from both pages, and compare what remains. Reusable pattern for any future PR that introduces a deliberate role-asymmetric element while preserving overall D4 visual parity.
  - **Insider hint must be structurally absent from Common, not just CSS-hidden:** the D2 spec says "Common never sees the hint". Implementing it as an always-rendered element with `display: none` for Common would technically pass a visual-only test but leak via DOM snippets/screenshot OCR. By branching on `role === "insider"` at the JSX level, the hint testid count is 0 for Common in any context — bullet-proof for both anti-cheat AND test assertions. Reusable pattern for any role-asymmetric subtle UI affordance.
  - **The D2 silence threshold anchors at startedAt, not at first-mount:** if the spec waits 5s between phase flip and assertion, "silence" is measured from `round.started_at` (the moment the asking phase began for everyone), not from when this client mounted. So a player who reloads mid-round and re-enters the asking shell gets the hint within seconds (if the group has been silent), not after another 30s reset. Implementation: `lastActivityMs = max(latest response.created_at, round.started_at)`. Aligns with Mid-round reconnect spec: "current phase + remaining time (computed from server `started_at` + `time_limit_s`)" — same shared anchor.
  - **AskingOther has its own Realtime channel with role suffix:** `supabase.channel(\`asking-other-\${roomId}-\${round}-\${role}\`)`. Per pattern note 26, channel names must be unique per subscriber instance to avoid Realtime fan-out collisions. The role suffix means Insider and Common (running in different browser contexts during e2e) get distinct channel names and won't share INSERT events. Reusable for any per-role component that subscribes to the same shared table.
  - **Cumulative Turbopack cold-compile flake amplifies per spec:** running 058+059+060 in a single `playwright test` invocation hangs the third spec at navigation even though each passes solo. The next-server processes show 110-130% CPU during compile and become unresponsive (curl timeout). Mitigation: run 1 spec at a time, or pre-warm via `bun run dev`. The flake is environmental (Next 16 Turbopack + Realtime channel warmup + 4-context concurrency) and matches the existing notes 23 & 68 — production behavior unchanged.

## 2026-05-09 — US-061 (Phase 5b.6 — Voting phase screen, D6 hidden tallies)
- TDD red: wrote `e2e/insider-voting.spec.ts` (1 multi-context test, 4 browser contexts via `createMultiRoleSession`). Test: 4-player setup → host start → wait roles + 'preparing' → identify Master via localStorage probe → Insider clicks ฉันพร้อมแล้ว → Master sees asking shell, taps "ทายถูกแล้ว" → poll DB phase='voting' (the auto-advance through 'guessed' celebration) → all 4 pages see `voting-phase-shell`, `voting-phase-tag` ('VOTING'), mm:ss `voting-deadline-timer`, `voting-header` ('WHO IS THE INSIDER?'), 4 `vote-target-card-<player_id>` testids, ZERO `vote-tally-*` testids (D6), `voting-progress` ('0 / 4') → host first-card height ≥120px → tap-toggle-deselect-then-switch contract on host page → all 4 pages cast a vote → poll DB count = 4 → poll DB phase = 'reveal'. First red run timed out at "Expected: voting Received: guessed" — the auto-advance RPC didn't exist yet, expected red.
- Migration `0030_advance_to_voting.sql` — new RPC `advance_to_voting(p_room_id, p_round, p_player_id)` per the C2.A guessed→voting edge. Standard Insider RPC skeleton: (1) `perform reconcile_round_phase`, (2) caller membership check (PGAME11/PG011), (3) phase-only `update game_insider_round set phase = 'voting' where ... and phase = 'guessed'` (idempotent via WHERE-clause filter, T-3.B "anyone advances"). `vote_deadline` and `eligible_voter_ids` were pre-populated by `mark_correct_guess` in migration 0025, so this RPC has no extra inputs.
- TS wrapper `advanceToVoting` added to `apps/insider/lib/insider-rpc.ts` next to the other Insider RPC wrappers, same `dispatch()`/camelCase-args/snake_case-RPC-args shape.
- New UI component `packages/ui/src/vote-target-card.tsx` — `<VoteTargetCard joinOrder displayName selected disabled testId onTap />`. Renders a 120px-tall full-tag-color player card with a goal-red ring + absolute ✓ overlay when selected. Tag-color palette mirrors `PlayerChip` (1-8 → red/blue/yellow/green/purple/orange/pink/cyan). Exported via `packages/ui/src/index.ts` barrel.
- New screen `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/voting.tsx` — accepts `initialPhase ∈ {'guessed', 'voting'}` and renders either: (a) a 2s celebration shell ('ทายถูก!' + 'GROUP GUESSED' + 'กำลังเปิดโหวต...') for `'guessed'`, scheduling `setTimeout(2000) → advanceToVoting()` from every client (idempotent RPC absorbs concurrent fires); (b) full Screen 7 voting UI for `'voting'` — header + 2-col grid of 4 vote-target-cards + 60s `vote_deadline` countdown (tabular-nums, error-red when ≤10s) + group progress ('X / Y voted'). Realtime subscriptions on both `game_insider_round` (phase + deadline + eligible_voter_ids) AND `game_insider_votes` (count). Selection state local; tap-toggle-deselect uses self-vote sentinel (re-tap same card calls `cast_vote(p_voted_player_id = mePlayerId)`) since the FK column requires a non-null target. Self-cards `disabled={isMeRow}` so the only path to self-vote is the toggle-deselect.
- Phase router in `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/lobby.tsx` extended with a `roundPhase ∈ {'guessed', 'voting'} → <Voting/>` branch in front of the existing `RoleReveal` fallthrough. Imports `Voting` next to the existing `RoleReveal`/`AskingPhase` imports.
- TDD green: `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-voting.spec.ts` → 1 passed (16.4s) on chromium.
- Regression spot-check: `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-asking-master.spec.ts` → 1 passed (12.5s) — the asking-phase contract for the Master view still holds.
- Lint: `bun run lint` → 3 packages cached/successful (3.4s). Realtime publication checker passes.
- Unit tests: `bunx vitest run` → 30 files / 193 tests passed (4.2s).
- Build: `bunx turbo run build --filter=@social-hub/insider` → succeeded (8.0s), 4 routes prerendered correctly.
- Typecheck: `cd apps/insider && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean; `cd packages/ui && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Test gotcha — host's first vote-target is host-self, which is disabled:** initial test had `allTargets[0].click()` for the toggle assertion, but on the host page `allTargets[0]` is the host's own card (sorted by join_order ascending). Since self-cards are `disabled={isMeRow}`, the click was a no-op and `aria-pressed` stayed false → 120s timeout. Fix: filter `pagePlayerIds` to exclude the host page and grab targets by `vote-target-card-${otherPlayerIds[i]}` testid. Reusable: any e2e exercising selection-toggle on a multi-card UI should look up cards by player_id, not array index.
  - **Master CAN vote (eligible_voter_ids includes them) — but cannot self-vote:** `mark_correct_guess` snapshots `eligible_voter_ids` from connected players including the master. So the master's voting card grid renders all 4 players, but the master's own card is disabled. The Insider also votes (as in the real Insider game where the Insider tries to throw suspicion elsewhere). The only voting restriction in the UI is "you can't vote for yourself" — server-side `cast_vote` will accept any `voted_player_id` so the self-disable is purely a UX guardrail.
  - **`game_insider_round` realtime payload includes `vote_deadline` + `eligible_voter_ids`:** migration 0017 publication column-list includes both, so a client subscribing to phase changes also gets the deadline + eligible array on the same payload — no separate fetch needed when phase flips to voting. Pattern: when adding a new realtime-driven phase screen that needs round metadata, check the publication column list (migration 0017 lines 73-84) before adding RPCs to fetch it.
  - **Self-vote sentinel for de-select instead of nullable FK:** `game_insider_votes.voted_player_id` is NOT NULL with a FK constraint, so "tap-toggle to de-select" can't write NULL. Pattern: store the voter's own player_id as the "deselected" sentinel value (re-tap calls `cast_vote(p_voted_player_id = mePlayerId)`), and on the client treat `voted_player_id === voter_player_id` as "no selection". Keeps the audit trail intact (last intent wins) without schema changes. Alternative considered: a "clear_vote" RPC that DELETEs the row, but DELETE rows aren't picked up by cast_vote's auto-advance count check, so toggling deselect could leak partial state. The self-sentinel keeps cast_vote's row-count semantics intact.

- `apps/insider/lib/insider-rpc.ts` — added `advanceToNextRound`, `getRevealedSecret` wrappers.
- New migration `0031_advance_to_next_round.sql` — anyone-advances RPC that flips `rooms.status: PLAYING → LOBBY` after reveal. Phase + status guards make it a no-op outside the reveal/result_failed window.
- New migration `0032_advance_to_reveal_idempotency_lock.sql` — fixed concurrent-fire scoring race in `advance_to_reveal` by adding `select ... for update` on the round row. Without this, 4 simultaneous reveal-screen mounts each applied the +2 score updates, multiplying total_score by 4-6×.
- New migration `0033_get_revealed_secret.sql` — public RPC that returns `secret_value` for any in-room player when `phase ∈ ('reveal','result_failed')`. Required because anon SELECT on `secret_value` is denied (column-RLS from US-038). PGAME21 = "secret not yet revealed", PGAME22 = "round not found".
- Regen `packages/types/src/database.types.ts`.
- **Verification:**
  - `e2e/insider-caught.spec.ts` ✓ (10.4s)
  - All Insider e2e: 10/10 in suite-run + the 1 cold-start flake retried clean (insider-lobby — known Next 16 Turbopack pattern; passes alone in 5.8s)
  - All Headball e2e: 14/14 ✓
  - `bunx vitest run`: 193 tests / 30 files ✓
  - Per-package typecheck (insider, headball, hub): clean
  - `bun run lint`: clean (3 packages cached/successful)
- **Learnings:**
  - The race-condition pattern in `advance_to_reveal` (read `scored_at`, then act, then stamp) was subtle — the WHERE-clause stamp guard masked it for serial callers. It only surfaced once US-062 mounted the RPC fire in 4 concurrent clients. Captured as a reusable rule in Codebase Patterns (top of file).
  - The `get_revealed_secret` RPC pattern complements US-021's `get_my_insider_secret` — Master/Insider during play, then ALL-players at reveal. Future asymmetric-secret games (Mafia / Spy / hidden roles) can lift this two-RPC shape directly.
  - "Anyone advances" + cumulative side effects = needs `for update` row lock. WHERE-clause idempotency only works for pure phase flips.

- Implemented Screen 8b dark-mode escaped reveal as a sibling branch in `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/reveal.tsx` (single component, branched on `caught`).

- Score badges: Insider row gets `+3` in warning-yellow w/ warning-tinted tile; everyone else 0 pts in surface tile. `roundDelta` already returned the escaped numbers from US-062 — reused it.
- Files changed:
  - `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/reveal.tsx` — added 8b variant block before existing 8a; added `myVote` memo for per-player ballot copy; tightened the "Insider, caught/escaped" inline string to the variant-correct word; updated header comment to cover both screens.
  - `e2e/insider-escaped.spec.ts` — new 4-context Playwright spec mirroring `insider-caught.spec.ts` shape, but voting strategy makes the Insider receive 0 votes (commonA tops the tally with 3) so `tally.topSet` excludes the insider's player_id and `caught=false`.
- Verification:
  - `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-escaped.spec.ts` → 1 passed (9.4s).
  - `cd apps/insider && bunx tsc --noEmit` → clean.
  - `bun run lint` → 3/3 packages cache-hit successful, realtime-publication checker green.
  - Browser dev-browser skill not available in autonomous context; the e2e bg-color assertion (`rgb(10, 14, 26)`) + testid asserts (`reveal-escaped-shell`, `reveal-secret-name`, `reveal-insider-badge` with "ESCAPED" text, `reveal-your-vote`, 4× `reveal-score-tile-*`, 4× `reveal-leader-row-*`, `reveal-next-round-cta`) constitute the visual contract verification per Ralph fallback policy.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Vote-routing trick to force ESCAPED outcome in tests:** the UI gates self-vote (handleTap disables `isMeRow`), so even with 4 players you can't simply have everyone vote for the Insider's neighbour and skip the insider. The cleanest 0-votes-for-Insider construction: master+insider+commonB all vote for commonA (commonA can't self-vote per UI, so commonA targets commonB). commonA gets 3 votes (top set = {commonA}), commonB gets 1, insider 0. Use this same pattern in US-064 if you ever need an "everyone misses" round in a different time-expired scenario.
  - **`voted_player_id === voter_player_id` is a "deselected" sentinel from voting.tsx**: the `myVote` memo treats self-votes as null (no ballot) instead of "You voted yourself", because the voting screen encodes the deselect action by writing the voter's own id into voted_player_id. If a future RPC ever stops accepting self-votes, this branch becomes dead but harmless.
  - **Tailwind v4 `text-on-dark/90` opacity escape:** the variant-internal copy uses `text-on-dark/90` for muted secondary text on tag-color backgrounds — the `/90` modifier works inside the runtime alpha channel without needing a pre-defined token. Reuse for any "muted-on-tag" text to avoid creating a parallel `--color-on-dark-soft` for every tag bg.

## 2026-05-09 16:48 — US-064 (Phase 5b.7c — Reveal screen TIME EXPIRED variant, Screen 8c)
- Added 3rd reveal variant for `phase='result_failed'` (asking-phase timeout). New e2e `e2e/insider-time-expired.spec.ts` drives a 4-context flow that backdates `started_at` via the service-role client + calls `expire_round` to flip phase → `result_failed`, then asserts Screen 8c renders for all clients with no scoring (everyone stays at 0) and the NEXT ROUND CTA flips `rooms.status` back to `LOBBY`.
- Files changed:
  - `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/reveal.tsx` — added a `phase: "reveal" | "result_failed"` prop, branched FIRST on `phase === "result_failed"` to render the new dark-shell + error-red-top-accent variant (data-testid `reveal-time-expired-shell`). The variant skips insider-was-X badge / scoreboard / leaderboard entirely and shows the secret + "No voting / No points" copy.
  - `apps/insider/app/room/[code]/lobby.tsx` — passes `phase={roundPhase}` to the Reveal mount so the component knows whether to render 8c vs 8a/8b.
  - `e2e/insider-time-expired.spec.ts` — new spec.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Time-expired e2e construction:** the cleanest deterministic way to force `phase='result_failed'` in a Playwright test is service-role `update started_at = now() - 10min` followed by `rpc('expire_round', { p_room_id, p_round })`. expire_round's WHERE-clause requires `now() >= started_at + time_limit_s`, so backdating started_at is mandatory; the default time_limit_s is 180/300/420 (per host-setup-form), not adjustable from the e2e UI flow. Don't try to wait the timer out — that would spend 3+ minutes per test.
  - **`phase` as the variant discriminator on the Reveal component:** caught-vs-escaped is a derived boolean from votes, but time-expired has no votes at all so the existing `caught` branch would falsely route to ESCAPED. Solution: pass the round phase explicitly as a prop and short-circuit before the `caught` derivation runs. Future variants (e.g. early-quit) should follow the same pattern — phase first, vote-derived branches second.
  - **Test isolation under Turbopack dev server:** running multiple Insider Playwright specs back-to-back via `playwright test e2e/insider-*.spec.ts` produces sporadic 120s timeouts on the room-creation `goto + waitForURL` step because the Turbopack dev server can hang on inter-test compile invalidation. Each spec passes individually after `pkill -f "next dev"; sleep 3`. Don't treat back-to-back failures as regressions without a fresh-server retry.
- Acceptance criteria:
  - ✓ Failing e2e first (time-expired-shell did not exist) → confirmed red.
  - ✓ Reveal screen time-expired variant renders per Screen 8c (dark bg + error-red top, TIME UP / ทายไม่ทันเวลา, secret revealed, no voting, no scoring).
  - ✓ Voting phase skipped entirely on timeout (lobby.tsx routes result_failed → Reveal directly; no Voting mount).
  - ✓ NEXT ROUND CTA visible to all 4 players (T-3.B "anyone advances"); click flips room.status to LOBBY.
  - ✓ e2e passes (12.0s on fresh server).
  - ✓ `bunx tsc --noEmit` (apps/insider) clean.
  - ✓ `bun run lint` (turbo, all 3 apps) clean.
  - ✓ `bunx vitest run` (workspace, 193 tests) clean.
  - ✓ Browser verification via the Playwright e2e (no separate dev-browser pass — Playwright IS the browser verification).

The "strictly more coverage with zero failures = pass" rule from US-013 generalizes for the 6th time now (Phases 0-5b). When marking a GATE, capture the actual count in the prd.json `notes` so future Ralph iterations don't re-investigate.
  - **Vercel-deferral cascade now spans 6 phases (0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5a, 5b).** Each gate has the same DEFERRED template. When US-005/006 unlock, the maintainer's one-time sweep will need: 7 baseline `/qa` runs (one per phase) saved to `.ralph/qa-baseline-{phase-N}.txt`, plus the one-shot manual 4-browser Insider round verification from US-065. The local proxy evidence (typecheck + lint + vitest + 3-app build + e2e) substitutes cleanly for now.
  - **Phase 5c kicks off next (10 edge-case e2e flows: voting tie, disconnected can be voted for can't vote, mid-round reconnect, late master action rejected, common→master_respond rejected, anon SELECT secret column rejected, race on expire_round, prefers-reduced-motion).** Per Phase 5 PRD, all 10 stories use the same `e2e/_helpers/multi-role.ts` + workspace-root playwright config established in 5b. No infra changes needed before US-066 starts.

## 2026-05-09 — US-066 (Phase 5c.1 — Voting tie e2e)
- Added `e2e/insider-voting-tie.spec.ts`: 4-context happy path that engineers a 2-2 tie at the top of the vote tally with the Insider IN the tied set, then asserts D2 "all tied counted as caught" resolution. Vote layout: Master+commonA → Insider (Insider gets 2 votes); Insider+commonB → commonA (commonA gets 2 votes). Insider can't self-vote (UI gate), so the Insider's deflection vote also feeds the tie.
- Test passes in 9.2s. DB tally invariant (Insider=2, commonA=2, top_count=2, top_set={Insider, commonA}) is asserted explicitly so the migration 0028 array_agg-of-max logic is verified end-to-end. Scoring contract: Master+2, commonA+2, commonB+2, Insider+0 — all 4 pages render Screen 8a (`reveal-caught-shell`) with CAUGHT badge + voted-by breakdown + 4 score tiles + 4 leaderboard rows + NEXT ROUND CTA.
- **Files changed:**
  - `e2e/insider-voting-tie.spec.ts` (new)
  - `.ralph/prd.json` (US-066 → passes: true + notes)
  - `.ralph/progress.txt` (this entry)
- **Learnings:**
  - **No new tie-handling code needed** — migration 0028 already implements D2 correctly via `array_agg(voted_player_id) where c = max(c)`. The existing unit test "tied vote with Insider in tied set → caught (D2)" (`migration-0028-advance-to-reveal.test.ts:261`) covers the SQL-level invariant; this e2e is the UI/Realtime end-to-end witness. **Reusable rule:** for "test-first" Ralph stories where the underlying state machine is already implemented + unit-tested, the e2e is purely a witness — no SQL/server changes, just a new spec under `e2e/` using the workspace multi-app webServer config from US-052.
  - **The story description's wording "both are marked caught" is about the top-voted SET, not about the visual outcome.** Migration 0028's tie path collapses into one of two outcome variants (CAUGHT or ESCAPED) based on whether the Insider's player_id is in the top-voted set. There is no third "tied" UI variant. So "voting tie" e2e really means: "4-player tie at top, assert the migration's set-membership branch is exercised". The spec asserts the explicit set membership via `topSet.has(insider) && topSet.has(commonA)` in addition to the visible CAUGHT shell.
  - **D4 caught-vs-escaped scoring is role-based, not vote-based** — every `role='player'` row gets +2 in the caught branch regardless of who voted for whom. So commonB (who voted for commonA, didn't vote for the Insider) still scores +2 because their role is 'player'. The e2e asserts all three non-Insider players score +2 even though only Master+commonA actually voted for the Insider.
  - **Insider's UI-gated self-vote constraint shapes tie engineering.** To engineer a 2-2 tie where the Insider is in the top set, the Insider must contribute the second vote to the OTHER tied target (commonA). That forces the layout: 2 voters → Insider, 1 voter (Insider) + 1 voter (commonB) → commonA. The constraint is anti-cheat-bulletproof — the test cannot bypass it (the vote-target-card for `playerId === mePlayerId` has `aria-pressed` blocked at the UI layer). Reusable: any future "engineer a specific tally" e2e must respect the no-self-vote rule when assigning the Insider's ballot.

## 2026-05-09 — US-067 (Phase 5c.2 — Disconnected player can be voted for, can't vote, T-4)

- Added `e2e/insider-voting-disconnect.spec.ts` — multi-role 4-page Playwright spec
  drives the T-4 "frozen eligible_voter_ids excludes disconnects but disconnected
  players still on player cards" contract end-to-end through the real Insider RPC
  stack + UI.
- Disconnect simulation: service-role `update players set connected = false`
  during the 'asking' phase, BEFORE the Master taps `master-mark-correct-cta`.
  mark_correct_guess (migration 0025) snapshots `eligible_voter_ids` from
  `connected = true` players, so the DB column is the source of truth — no need
  to actually close the disconnected player's browser context.
- Files changed: `e2e/insider-voting-disconnect.spec.ts` (new, ~270 lines)
- Test verifies:
  1. eligible_voter_ids has 3 entries post-guess; the disconnected Common is NOT in the array.
  2. On the 3 still-connected pages (Master, Insider, live Common), the disconnected
     Common's `vote-target-card-${disconnectedPid}` IS visible — they remain a valid
     suspect (T-4 "still on player cards"). All 4 cards render on every voter's screen.
  3. After 3 eligible voters cast votes, phase auto-advances to 'reveal' WITHOUT waiting
     for the disconnected player's ballot. cast_vote's auto-advance counts only votes
     where voter_player_id ∈ eligible_voter_ids, so 3/3 triggers the flip.
  4. game_insider_votes contains exactly 3 rows; voter_player_ids does NOT include
     the disconnected Common.
- Test result: ✓ passes in 9.0s (workspace-root playwright config, all 3 dev servers
  spawned via Turbo).
- Verification: typecheck (`bunx tsc --noEmit -p e2e/tsconfig.json`) clean. Browser
  verification implicit — 4-context Playwright run IS the browser verification per
  the in-context Ralph instruction "If no browser tooling is available in this
  autonomous context: write Playwright e2e specs that assert the UI behavior. The
  e2e specs ARE the verification."

- **Learnings:**
  - The "disconnect during asking" simulation pattern: don't try to close the
    browser context — just flip `players.connected = false` via service-role.
    The DB column is what mark_correct_guess reads when snapshotting
    eligible_voter_ids. The disconnected player's page is still alive and
    subscribes to realtime, but `isEligible = eligibleIds.includes(mePlayerId)`
    is false, so handleTap early-returns and cast_vote rejects with PGAME17 if
    they ever attempted. Both layers protect the invariant — UI gate + RPC gate.
  - Vote-target rendering reads from the `players` table, NOT
    `eligible_voter_ids`. A disconnected player still appears as a vote target
    card to other players. Only the disconnect-ed player's own ability to vote
    is gated. This is the critical T-4 distinction: who you can VOTE FOR ≠ who
    is ELIGIBLE TO VOTE.
  - cast_vote's auto-advance condition is
    `count(votes WHERE voter_player_id = ANY(eligible)) >= len(eligible)`.
    That's why the round advances on 3 ballots when only 3 are eligible —
    not because of any "disconnect-aware" branching, but because the eligible
    set was frozen at 3 at vote-phase entry.
