## 2026-05-09T00:00:00Z — US-068
Phase 5c.3 — Test-first: Mid-round reconnect during asking phase.
- Added `e2e/insider-asking-reconnect.spec.ts` (1 test, 39.3s) covering the full reconnect contract: 4-player room → asking phase → Master records 5 responses via `master_respond` RPC → drop a Common player by closing their browser context → wait 30s real time → reconnect via fresh context with `localStorage.insider_player_id` pre-seeded via `addInitScript` → assert (a) lands on `asking-phase-shell` (not JoinView/Master/Insider), (b) timer reflects elapsed wall-clock from `started_at` (strictly less than `time_limit_s`, ±3s of expected, decreases over 1.5s), (c) all 5 historical responses present in `asking-other-feed-row` count.
- Files changed:
  - `e2e/insider-asking-reconnect.spec.ts` (new)
  - `.ralph/prd.json` (US-068 passes:true)
- Verified: `bunx playwright test e2e/insider-asking-reconnect.spec.ts` ✓; `bunx tsc --noEmit -p e2e/tsconfig.json` ✓; `cd apps/insider && bunx tsc --noEmit` ✓
- **Learnings:**
  - **Reconnect simulation pattern (durable):** the right primitive is `browser.newContext()` + `context.addInitScript((pid) => window.localStorage.setItem("insider_player_id", pid), playerId)` BEFORE `newPage().goto(...)`. `addInitScript` runs on every navigation BEFORE page scripts, so the lobby's first `useEffect` reads the seeded id and resolves `me` immediately, bypassing `JoinView`. Closing the original `dropPage.context()` is sufficient to model a phone going offline — the persisted `players` row is what makes the new context authoritative as the same player. NEVER reuse the original context: it still has live websocket subscriptions and would leak realtime channels into the reconnect assertions.
  - **Timer-from-server-anchor verification idiom:** to prove a timer is computed from `started_at + time_limit_s` rather than re-seeded on mount, assert (a) `firstReading < timeLimitS` (hard proof of NOT-reset), (b) `|firstReading - expectedRemainingS| <= 3s` where `expectedRemainingS = floor(timeLimitS - (Date.now() - startedAtMs) / 1000)`, AND (c) `secondReading < firstReading` after a ~1.5s wait (proves the tick is alive after reconnect, not stale). Single-reading checks miss the case where a stale localStorage cached the timer value.
  - **Negative testid matrix as role assertion:** the AskingOther DOM is shared by Insider+Common but differs by ONE testid (`asking-other-insider-hint`). To prove a reconnected Common landed on the right view without coupling to Insider/Common branching, assert presence of `asking-other-feed` AND absence of three negative testids: `insider-join-name-input` (NOT JoinView), `master-response-buttons` (NOT Master), `asking-other-insider-hint` (NOT Insider). Catches phase routing bugs that would otherwise pass a single-positive-testid check.
  - **Driving response history via RPC, not UI clicks:** for "the dropped player must see N historical responses", call `supabase.rpc("master_respond", {...})` directly with the master's `p_player_id`. master_respond is `SECURITY DEFINER` granted to anon, so the service-role test client can call it (anon RLS doesn't block SECURITY DEFINER). Adding 5 UI clicks would burn ~5s for zero extra coverage — the response-button click path is already covered by `insider-asking-master.spec.ts`.
  - **Wait for the live-control feed to render before dropping:** asserting `liveCommonPage.getByTestId("asking-other-feed-row").toHaveCount(5)` before closing the drop context is the proof that the 5 RPC inserts actually committed AND the realtime channel propagated them. Without this barrier, a flaky drop could happen before the inserts land in the DB, leaving the reconnecting client to see 0 rows for entirely different reasons.
---

## 2026-05-09T17:35Z — US-069
- Wrote e2e/insider-late-guess-rejected.spec.ts (Phase 5c.4) — proves mark_correct_guess server-side time guard end-to-end:
  * 4-player room, host → asking phase begins
  * service-role backdates started_at by 10min AFTER master mounts AskingMaster (cached startedAt prop keeps CTA enabled — models click just-before-server-deadline)
  * Master clicks ทายถูกแล้ว → reconcile flips asking→result_failed inside TX → mark_correct_guess sees v_phase='result_failed' → raises PG002 → TX rolls back
  * UI shows guessError "หมดเวลาแล้ว" via mapGuessError
  * DB phase still 'asking' post-click (rollback per pattern note 40); scored_at still null
  * test fires expire_round (service role) to commit the deferred reconcile
  * lobby.tsx routes phase='result_failed' → Reveal screen — assert reveal-time-expired-shell visible on master + all clients, secret revealed, no insider-badge / no score-tiles
- Files changed: `e2e/insider-late-guess-rejected.spec.ts` (new), `.ralph/prd.json`, `.ralph/progress.txt`
- Spec passes (6.0s); typecheck clean. Re-ran insider-asking-master / insider-time-expired / insider-voting — all green; no regressions.
- **Learnings:**
  - Spec-criterion variant: "round timer set to 5s for testing" not feasible without a schema escape hatch — `create_insider_room` enforces `time_limit_s ∈ {180,300,420}` (PGAME20 / migration 0029). Service-role `update game_insider_round set started_at = now() - 10min` simulates the same condition with no test-only schema change. Pattern: when an acceptance criterion suggests a config value outside the validated range, prefer service-role row mutation that achieves the same temporal contract.
  - `master-mark-correct-cta` is intentionally NOT gated on `remainingS <= 0` (only the response yes/no/unsure buttons are) — design lets the Master "click at the moment the timer hits 0" and reach the server guard rather than a silent client no-op. The client-side guard would otherwise mask the server-side PGAME02 path entirely. Document this in pattern note for future test authors who need to exercise client-side disabled vs. server-side rejected boundary.
  - Cwd matters for Playwright: when `pwd` is inside `e2e/`, `bunx playwright test e2e/...` against the workspace-root `playwright.config.ts` flakes the `webServer.reuseExistingServer:true` flow (it can't resolve relative paths consistently). Always invoke `bunx playwright test` from workspace root. Future iterations: don't `cd e2e` and forget to come back — symptom is the test failing at `waitForURL` after `create-insider-room-cta` click on what is otherwise a fully working UI.
- **Codebase Pattern (added):**
  - **Server-side time-guard E2E pattern (per US-069):** to verify "RPC X rejects after deadline" without waiting time_limit_s seconds, (1) reach the phase that exposes the guarded action, (2) service-role backdate the round's started_at column AFTER the page that owns the action has mounted (its props are cached), (3) click the UI control normally — server reconciles + raises the deadline-specific errcode, transaction rolls back per pattern note 40, (4) assert UI shows the mapped error message AND DB phase is unchanged (proves rollback), (5) fire the public reconcile RPC (expire_round / etc.) explicitly to commit the deferred phase flip, (6) assert UI re-routes to the post-deadline screen. Reusable for any future "late action rejected" e2e (PGAME19 cast_vote-after-vote-deadline, etc.).

## 2026-05-09T17:38 — US-070
- Implemented Phase 5c.5 — Common attempts master_respond → rejected.
- Files changed:
  - `apps/insider/lib/__tests__/us-070-common-master-respond-denied.test.ts` — new story-focused vitest integration test. Builds an Insider room with Master + Insider + 2 Commons in `phase='asking'`, then has a Common (`role='player'`) call `master_respond('yes')` directly via the anon Supabase client. Asserts `error.code === 'PG015'`, message matches `/PGAME15/` and `/only master can respond/`, no row in `game_insider_responses`, phase still `'asking'`. In-test sanity baseline immediately after: Master calls the SAME RPC on the SAME fixture and succeeds (response='yes' written) — proving the rejection above is role-driven, not fixture- or schema-driven.
  - `.ralph/prd.json` — passes:true with notes.
- Verification:
  - `bunx vitest run lib/__tests__/us-070-common-master-respond-denied.test.ts` — 1/1 pass.
  - `bunx vitest run` (apps/insider) — 14 files, 91/91 pass (no regressions).
  - `bunx tsc --noEmit` (apps/insider) — clean.
  - `bun run lint` (workspace) — 3/3 packages successful (insider re-linted).
- **Learnings:**
  - **Story-focused regression tests vs. broad migration tests (per US-070 vs. existing migration-0024-master-respond.test.ts):** the migration-0024 test (US-045) was written when the RPC first landed and exercises ALL its rejection branches (insider+player → PGAME15, preparing/guessed → PGAME16, expired → PGAME02, happy path). Phase 5c regression-coverage stories like US-070 add a SECOND story-focused test that targets ONE specific contract from ONE specific actor's POV (here: Common → PGAME15). Pattern: name the file `us-NNN-<short-contract>.test.ts`, single `describe("US-NNN — <story title>")`, ideally a single `it()` with both the negative assertion AND a positive control. The control prevents false-greens caused by fixture or schema drift — if the RPC is broken end-to-end (e.g., always rejects, or always accepts), exactly one of the two assertions in the same test fails, which is more diagnostic than a one-sided assertion. Reusable for any future Phase 5c.x integration-test story (US-5c.6 anon SELECT secret, US-5c.7 expire_round race, etc.).
  - **In-test contract-positive-control pattern (per US-070):** to harden a "X is rejected" assertion against false greens, end the same `it()` block with the positive control — same RPC, same fixture, same arguments EXCEPT the one piece that flips the contract (here: caller's player_id flips from a Common to the Master). If both assertions hold, the contract is bound to the variable under test, not to incidental fixture state. Cheaper than two separate it() blocks (no second fixture build) and stronger than a one-sided assertion. Caveat: the positive control mutates the fixture (writes a response row), so any further assertions in the same test must account for that. For US-070 the order is: assert no rows after Common call → assert one row after Master call.

## 2026-05-09T10:42Z — US-071
- Phase 5c.6 — Anon SELECT secret_value column → permission denied (column-level GRANT verified, A1.C).
- Files changed: apps/headball/lib/__tests__/insider-secret-rls.test.ts (new)
- Test setup: service-role inserts a `rooms` row (code TST071, game_type insider) and a
  `game_insider_round` row referencing the seeded `football-premier-league` content_pack.
  Test asserts (a) anon `select secret_value` → SQLSTATE 42501, (b) anon `select *` →
  42501 (`*` expands to include the ungranted column), (c) anon explicit non-secret
  select succeeds with `secret_value` absent from the row.
- Verification: `bunx vitest run lib/__tests__/insider-secret-rls.test.ts` → 3/3 passed.
  `bunx tsc --noEmit` → clean.
- **Learnings:**
  - **Vitest pattern for column-level RLS verification:** when a migration uses `revoke
    select ... from anon` + targeted `grant select (col1, col2, ...) to anon`, supabase-js
    surfaces the underlying Postgres SQLSTATE on `error.code` (`'42501'` for
    insufficient_privilege). Both explicit-column SELECT of a denied column AND `select
    *` produce the same code (PostgREST translates the column list into an explicit
    SELECT, but `*` expands to all table columns including the ungranted ones).
  - **Self-contained RLS test fixtures:** since `rooms.id` cascades via FK to
    `game_insider_round`, a single `delete from rooms where code = ...` in afterAll
    cleans up the whole fixture — no need to also delete from game_insider_round /
    game_insider_roles. Same pattern reusable for any future
    `game_insider_<x>` RLS test.
  - **Pack slug for fixtures:** `'football-premier-league'` is always seeded via
    migration 0013, so it's the safe FK target for any test that just needs a valid
    `pack_slug` (avoids needing to seed a fresh content_packs row like
    get-random-pack-item.test.ts does).
